prastāvanā
āja maiṃ priya vijña pāṭhakoṃke sāmane munidharmakā mahān graṃtha śrīmūlācāra saṃskṛtachāyā aura hiṃdībhāṣāṭīkāsahita upasthita karatā hūṃ| isameṃ munidharmakī sabakriyāyeṃ bahuta vistārase varṇana kī gaī haiṃ | isameṃ bāraha adhikāra haiṃ
mūlaguṇādhikāra, bṛhatpratyākhyānasaṃstarastavādhikāra, saṃkṣepapratyākhyānādhikāra, samācārādhikāra, paṃcācārādhikāra, piṃḍaśuddhiadhi. kāra, ṣaḍāvaśyakādhikāra, dvādaśānuprekṣādhikāra, anagārabhāvanādhikāra, samayasārādhikāra, śīlaguṇādhikāra, paryāptiadhikāra | ina adhikāroṃkā jaisā nāma hai usīke anusāra kathana kiyā gayā hai|
abataka munidharmakā koī graṃtha prakāśita nahīṃ huā thā isa kāraṇa bahutase bhavyajīvoṃko munidharmakī kriyāoṃke svarūpakā jñāna hī nahīṃ thā | aba bhāgyodayase munianaṃtakīrti di0 jaina graṃthamālāne bhavya jīvoṃke upakārārtha isa mahān graṃthako prakāśita kiyā hai | isa mahāna graṃthake mūlakartā śrīvaṭṭakerakhāmī haiṃ | isa graṃthakī saṃskṛtaṭīkā ācāravṛttike kartā śrīvasunaṃdisiddhāṃtacakravartī haiṃ | dūsarī mūlācāra pradīpaka saṃskṛtaṭīkā śrīsakalakīrti

﻿________________

ācāryane bhī banāī hai aura pahalī ācāravṛtti saṃskṛtaṭīkāke anusāra jaipurī deśabhāṣā ṭīkā paṃ0 naṃdalālajī jaipuranivāsīne ādhī 516 gāthā taka banāī usake bāda ukta paṃḍitajīkā khargavāsa hogayā | paścāt paṃ0 ṛṣabhadāsajīne avaśiṣṭa ādhī vanāke usaṭīkāko pūrṇa kiyā| usakeviṣayameṃ "ṭīkā deśabhāṣāmaya prāraṃbhī su naṃdalāla pūraṇa karī ṛṣabhadāsa yaha niradhāra hai" aisā bhāṣākārakā kavittabhī hai| jainamatameṃ mokṣa munidharmase hī hai isaliye mokṣakeliye yahī graṃtha sākṣāt upayogī hosakegā | yaha bhāṣāṭīkā ukta bhāṣāṭīkāke anusāra hī kī gaī hai | aba hama viśeṣa na likhakara kevala itanā hī kahate haiṃ ki isa graṃthamālā
ke saṃrakṣaka śrīmān seṭha sukhānaṃdajīne jo isa graṃthakā uddhāra karāyā hai usake liye koṭiśaḥ dhanyavāda hai aura āśā karate haiṃ ki ukta seṭha sāhaba isake phaṃḍake vaḍhāne meṃ apanī udāratākā paricaya dete raheṃge| _
aṃtameṃ prārthanā hai ki isa graṃthake saṃpādana va saṃśodhana karane meṃ jo truṭiyāṃ rahagaī hoṃ unako khādhyāyapremī sajjanagaṇa śuddhakara mere ūpara kṣamā karate hue svādhyāya kareṃ | ityalaṃ vijñeṣu | jainagraṃthauddhārakakāryālaya } jinavāṇīkā sevaka khattaragalī haudāvāḍa़ī .!
paṃ. manoharalāla po. giragāṃva-baṃbaī kārtikavadi 14 saṃ0 1976 pāḍhama (mainapurī) nivāsī

﻿________________

munianaṃtakīrti di0 jaina - graṃthamālā
1 yaha graṃthamālā svargīya munianaṃtakīrtijīke smaraṇārtha kholī - gaī hai | isameṃ prācīna ārṣagraṃthoṃkā uddhāra karāyā jāyagā | isake saṃrakṣaka śrīmān seṭha gurumukharāya sukhānaṃdajī haiṃ |
2 munimahārājake nāmase khulanekā kāraṇa yaha hai ki eka samaya munimahārāja bhramaṇa karate hue mumbaīnagara meṃ padhāre | eka dina yahāṃke suprasiddha ukta seṭha sukhānaṃdajīke yahāṃ muni mahārājakā āhāra navadhā bhakti ke sātha nirvighna huā | usake harṣameṃ seṭha sāhabane apanī udāratākā paricaya deneke liye 1101 ) gyāraha sau eka rupaye munijīke nāmase jainagraṃtha uddhāra karāne ke liye dānameṃ diye | munimahārāja phira bhramaṇa karate hue murainā nagara meṃ padhāre aura rogase grasita hojānese vahāṃ unakā svargavāsa hogayā | usake kucha dinoṃ bāda una gyāraha sau eka rupaye se munidharmakā mahān graṃtha mūlācāra hiṃdī bhāṣā ṭīkā sahita munimahārājake nāmase prakāśita kiyā gayā hai |
3 isameṃ jitane graṃtha prakāśita hoṃge unakā mūlya lāgatamātra rakkhā jāyagā | lāgatameṃ graṃtha saṃpādana karāī, saṃśodhana karāī chapāī, jilda baṃdhavāī āphisakharca aura kamīśana bhī śāmila
samajhā jāyagā |
nivedaka
miti kārtika sudi paṃ0 manoharalāla śāstrī khattaralī haudāvāḍa़ī po0 giragāṃva baṃbaī |
14 saṃ0 1976

gāthā
saṃsāracakkavālam sārago laga savvamidaṃ uvadesaṃ ..
samaṇo metti ya paḍhamaṃ
sīle vimaridavvaṃ
savvaṃ pāṇāraṃbha
sammaṃ me savvabhūde savvaṃ āhāravihaṃ savvaṃ āhāravihiṃ, paśca samadā sāmācāro... saṃjamaṇāṇuva karaṇe ..
saṃjamatavaguṇasīlā sukhadukkhe uvayāro
sacchaṃdagadāgadasaya sissāṇuggahakusalo saṃgahaṇuggahakusalo
saṃthāravāsayāṇaṃ sevālapaṇaya keṇaga...

namaḥ paramātmane | śrīvaṭṭakerakhāmīkṛta mūlācāra|
(utthānikāchāyāhiṃdībhāṣāṭīkā sahita)
mūlaguṇādhikāra || 1||
dohā-vaṃdauṃ śrījinasiddhapada, ācārajauvajhāya |
sādhudharmajinabhāratī, jinagrahacaityasahāya || vaṭṭakerakhāmī praṇami, nami vasunaṃdīsari |
mūlācāra vicārikeṃ, bhāṣauṃ lakhi guṇabhūri || āge 'mūlagraṃthakāra maṃgalācaraṇapūrvaka mūlaguṇoṃke kahanekī pratijñā karate haiṃ;

mūlaguṇādhikāra 1 |
ṛṣabhādijinavarāṇāṃ nāmaniruktiṃ guṇānukīrti ca | kṛtvā arcayitvā ca triśuddhapraṇāmaḥ stavo jñeyaḥ || 24 || artha — ṛṣabha ajita ādi cauvīsa tīrthaṃkaroṃke nāmakī nirukti arthāt nāmake anusāra arthakaranā, unake asādhāraṇa guṇako pragaṭa karanā, unake caraṇayugalako pūjakara manavacanakā - kī śuddhatā se stuti karanā use caturviṃśatistava kahate haiṃ || 24 || āge vaṃdanākā kharūpa kahate haiṃ:
11
arahaṃtasiddhapaḍimātavasudguṇagurugurūṇa rādīṇaṃ | kidikammeṇidareṇa ya tiyaraṇasaṃkocaṇaṃ paṇamo || 25 ||
arahaṃtasiddhapratimātapaḥśrutaguṇagurugurūṇāṃ rādhīnām | kṛtakarmaṇā itareṇa ca trikaraṇasaṃkocanaṃ praṇāmaḥ || 25 || artha - arahaṃta pratimā, siddhapratimā, anaśanādi bāraha tapoṃkara adhika tapaguru, aṃgapūrvādirūpa āgamajñānase adhika śrutaguru, vyākaraṇa nyāya ādi jñānakī viśeṣatārūpa guṇoṃkara adhika guṇaguru, apane ko dīkṣādenevāle dīkṣāguru aura bahutakālake dīkṣita rādhikaguru - inako kāyotsargādika siddhabhakti gurubhakti rūpa kriyākarmase tathā śrutabhakti ādi kriyāke vinā mastaka namānerūpa muṃḍavaṃdanākara mana vacana kāyakī śuddhise namaskāra karanā vaha vaṃdanā nāmā mūlaguṇa hai || 25 ||
āge pratikramaṇakā svarūpa kahate haiṃ:
davveṃ khette kāle bhāve ya kidāvarāhasohaṇayaṃ | niṃdaṇagarahaṇajutto maṇavacakāyeṇa paḍikamaṇaṃ || 26 ||

dravye kṣetre kāle bhāve ca kṛtāparādhaśodhanam | . niṃdanagarhaṇayukto manovaca:kāyena pratikramaṇam || 26 ||
artha-āhāra śarīrādi dravyameṃ, vasatikā śayana āsana ādi kṣetrameṃ, prātaḥkāla ādi kālameṃ, cittake vyāpārarūpa bhāva (pariṇāma )meṃ kiyā gayā jo vratameṃ doṣa usakā śubha mana vacana kāyase śodhanā, apane doṣako apane āpa pragaṭakaranā, ācāryādikoṃke samīpa ālocanāpūrvaka apane doṣoṃko pragaṭa karanā vaha munirājake pratikramaṇa guṇa hotā hai || 26 ||
āge pratyākhyānakā kharūpa kahate haiṃ;ṇāmādīṇaṃ chaṇṇaṃ ajoggaparivajaṇaṃ tikaraṇeṇa | pañcakkhāṇaṃ ṇeyaṃ aṇāgayaṃ cāgame kāle ||27|| nāmādīnāṃ ṣaṇṇāṃ ayogyaparivarjanaṃ trikaraṇaiḥ | pratyākhyānaṃ jñeyaṃ anāgataṃ cāgame kāle || 27 ||
artha-nāma sthāpanā dravya kṣetra kāla bhāva ina chahoṃmeṃ śubha mana vacana kāyase āgāmī kālake liye ayogyakā tyāga karanā arthāt ayogya nāma nahīṃ karūṃgā, na kahūṃgā aura na ciṃtavana karūṃgā ityādi tyāgako pratyākhyāna jānanā || 27 || ___ āge kāyotsargakā svarūpa kahate haiṃ;devassiyaṇiyamādisu jahuttamāṇeṇa uttakālamhi | jiṇaguṇaciṃtaṇajutto kāosaggo taṇu visaggo ||28||

mūlācāra
artha-kapāsa reśama roma tīnake bane hue vastra, mṛgachālā ādi carma, vṛkṣādikī chālase utpanna sana ādike ṭāṭa, athavā pattā tṛṇa ādi-inase śarīrakā ācchādana nahīṃ karanā, kaḍa़e hāra ādi ābhūṣaṇoṃse bhūṣita na honā, saṃyamake vināśaka dravyoṃkara rahita honā-aisā tīnajagatakara pūjya vastrādi-bāhyaparigraharahita acelakavata mūlaguṇa hai || 30 || isase hiṃsākā upārjanarūpadoṣa, prakṣālanadoṣa, yācanādidoṣa nahīṃ hote |

āge munirājake chaha kāla hote haiṃ unameṃse ātmasaṃskārakāla saṃllekhanākāla uttamārthakāla ye tīna kāla to ārādhanāmeṃ varṇana kiye jāyeṃge aura śeṣa dīkṣākāla, śikṣākāla, gaṇapoṣaṇakāla ye tīna kāla ācārameṃ varṇana kiye jāyeṃge| inameṃse ādike tīna kālameṃ jo maraṇakā avasara ājāya to aisā karanā cāhiye|
savvadukkhappahīṇāṇaṃ siddhāṇaṃ arahado ṇamo| saddahe jiṇapaṇṇattaṃ pañcakkhāmi ya pāvayaṃ || 37||

āge bhaktike prakarṣakeliye phira namaskāra karate haiṃ;ṇamotthu dhudapāvāṇaṃ siddhāṇaṃ ca mahesiṇaṃ | saṃtharaṃ paḍivajāmi jahā kevalidesiyaṃ || 38 ||
namostu dhutapāpebhyaḥ siddhebhyaḥ ca maharṣibhyaḥ |
saṃstaraṃ pratipadye yathā kevalideśitam || 38 || artha-jinhoṃne pāpakarma naṣṭa karadiye aise siddha parameṣṭhī tathā kevala ṛddhiko prāpta ahaṃta parameṣṭhī ina donoṃko namaskāra hove,

22
mūlācāra
hojātā hai taba akelā hī siddha ( mukta ) hotā hai | bhāvārthayaha jīva saba kāla aura saba avasthāoṃmeṃ akelā hī hai ||47|| ego me sassado appā ṇāṇadaṃsaṇalakkhaṇo | sesā me bāhirā bhāvā savve saṃjogalakkhaṇā ||48||

artha-jñānadarśana lakṣaṇavālā eka merā ātmā hī nitya hai, śeṣa śarīrādika mere bāhya padārtha haiṃ ve ātmāke saṃyogasaṃbaṃdhase utpanna haiṃ isaliye vināśīka haiṃ || 48||
āge kahate haiṃ ki saṃyogalakṣaṇabhāvakā tyāga kyoṃ karanā cāhiye usakā uttara kahate haiṃsaṃjoyamūlaṃ jīveṇa pattā dukkhaparaṃparā| tamhā saṃjogasaṃbaṃdhaṃ savvaṃ tiviheṇa vosare ||49||
saṃyogamūlaṃ jīvena prāptā duḥkhaparaṃparā | tasmāt saṃyogasaṃbaṃdhaṃ sarva trividhena vyutsṛjāmi || 49 ||
artha-isa jīvane paradravyake sātha saṃyogake nimittase hameśā duḥkha bhoge isaliye saba saṃyoga saṃbaṃdhako mana vacana kāya-ina tīnoṃse choḍa़tā hūṃ || 49||
āge phira bhī duścaritrake tyāgakeliye kahate haiṃ;mūlaguṇauttaraguṇe jo meṃ ṇārādhido pamādeṇa | tamahaṃ savvaṃ ṇiṃde paḍikkame āgamissāṇaṃ ||50||

bṛhatpratyākhyānasaṃstarastavādhikāra 2|| 23 mūlaguṇottaraguṇeṣu yo mayā na ārādhitaḥ pramādena | tamahaṃ sarva niṃdāmi pratikramāmi āgamiṣyati || 50 ||
artha-mūlaguṇa (pradhānaguṇa ) aura uttara (viśeṣa) guṇaina donoṃ prakārake guṇoṃmeṃse jinakā maiṃne ālasyakara ārādhana (sevana ) nahīṃ kiyā una saba apane doṣoṃkī maiṃ niṃdā karatā hūṃ, tathā āgāmī kālameṃ jo guṇa ārādhanemeṃ na āveṃ unake doṣoṃkī bhī niṃdā karatā hūṃ aura pratikramaṇa ( tyāga ) karatā hūṃ || 50 || assaṃjamamaṇṇāṇaṃ micchattaṃ savvameva ya mamattiṃ | jīvesu ajīvesu ya taṃ śiṃde taṃ ca garihāmi ||51||
asaṃyamamajñānaṃ mithyātvaṃ sarvameva ca mamatvaṃ | . jīveṣvajīveṣu ca tat niṃdāmi taca garhe ||51||
artha-pāpake kāraṇa asaṃyamabhāva, śraddhānarahita vastukā jānanārūpa ajñāna bhāva, - aśraddhānarūpa mithyātvabhāva, aura jīva tathā ajīvapadārthoṃ meṃ mamatābhāva-aise saba bhāvoṃkī maiṃ niṃdā karatā hūṃ tathā gardā karatā hūṃ arthāt unake doṣoṃko prakaṭa karatā hūṃ ||51||
āge koī praśnakare ki pramādase doṣa lage haiṃ unakā to tyāga kiyā paraṃtu pramādoṃkā tyāga kyoṃ nahīṃ kiyā usakā samādhāna kahate haiṃsatta bhae aṭṭha mae saṇṇā cattāri gārave tiṇṇi | tettīsadāsaṇāo rāyaddosaṃ ca garihāmi || 52||

mūlācārasapta bhayāni aṣṭau madān saṃjñāścatasraḥ gauravāṇi trīṇi | trayastriṃśadāsādanāṃ rāgadveṣau ca garhe || 52 ||
artha-sāta bhaya, āṭhamada, āhāra bhaya maithuna parigraha-inakī abhilāṣārūpa cāra saṃjñā, ṛddhikā garvarūpa ṛddhigaurava-rasagauravasāta (sukha ) gaurava-aise tīna gaurava, tetīsa padārthoṃ kī āsādanā (paribhava ), prītirūpa rāga aura aprītirūpa dveṣa-ina saba bhāvoṃkā maiṃ ācaraṇa nahīṃ karatā tyāga karatā hūṃ || 52 ||
unameṃse prathama sāta bhaya aura āṭha madoṃko kahate haiṃiha paraloyattāṇaṃ aguttimaraṇaṃ ca veyaṇākassi bhayā| viṇṇāṇissariyāṇā kulabalatavarūvajāi mayā ||3|| ihaparalokau atrāṇaṃ aguptimaraṇaṃ vedanā ākasikaṃ bhayāni | vijñānamaiśvarya ājñā kulabalataporūpajātiḥ madāḥ ||53||
artha-isalokabhaya, paralokabhaya, arakṣākā bhaya, gupta rahaneke sthāna (gaḍha-kilā) na honekā bhaya, maranekā bhaya, śarīrādikī pīḍākā bhayarūpa vedanābhaya, vinā kāraṇa meghagarjanādikase utpanna huā ākasmikabhaya-ye sāta bhaya haiṃ | gaṇita kāvya gaṃdharva saṃgītādi vidyākā abhimānakharūpa vijñānamada, dhanakuṭuṃba ādi bāhya saṃpadākā abhimānarūpa aiśvaryamada, vacanake ullaṃghana na honerūpa ājñāmada, pitā pitāmahake uttama ikṣvāku ādi vaṃśameṃ janma honerūpa kulakā mada, śarīrakī śaktike abhimānarūpa balamada, kāyako saṃtāpa denekā ahaṃkārarūpa tapomada, śarīrakī suṃdaratā lāvaṇyatākā abhimānakharūpa rūpamada, mātākī pakṣakī pari.

bṛhatpratyākhyānasaṃstarastavādhikāra 2| 27 rameṃ, cāritrācārameṃ-isataraha cāroṃ ārādhanāoṃmeṃ acala (dṛḍha) ho tathā dhairyaguṇa sahita ho, apane aura paramatake śāstroṃke vicārameṃ catura ho, aura ekāṃtameṃ ālocanā kiye gaye gupta ācaraṇoṃko kisīse kahanevālā na ho aisā ācārya hotā hai| usīke pāsa ālocanā karanī cāhiye || 57 || ___ āge ālocanāke vāda kṣamāvanā karanekā vidhāna kahate haiṃ;rāgeṇa va doseṇa va jaṃ meṃ akadaṇhuyaṃ pamādeṇa | jo meṃ kiṃcivi bhaṇio tamahaṃ savvaṃ khamāvemi ||58|| rāgeṇa vā dveṣeṇa vā yat mayā akṛtajñatvaṃ pramādena | yat mayā kiṃcidapi bhaṇitaṃ tadahaṃ sarva kṣamayāmi ||58||
artha-māyā lobha sneharūpa rāgakara tathā krodha māna aprītirūpa dveṣakara jo maiṃne akṛtajñapanā kiyā arthāt tumhāre sātha ayogya vartāva kiyā aura pramādase jo kucha bhī anucita kisīko kahā ho usake liye maiṃ saba janoṃse kṣamā māṃgatā hūṃ tathā maiṃ kṣamā karatā hūṃ saba jīvoṃko saṃtuṣṭa karatā hūṃ.|| 58 ||
aise kṣamābhāvakara kṣapaka saṃnyāsa karanekī abhilāṣākara ācāryoṃko maraṇake bheda pūchatā hai usakā uttara kahate haiṃ;tivihaṃ bhaṇiyaṃ maraṇaṃ bālāṇaṃ bālapaṃḍiyāṇaṃ ca | taiyaṃ paṃḍiyamaraṇaṃ jaṃ kevaliṇo aṇumaraṃti || 59|| . trividhaṃ bhaṇitaṃ maraṇaṃ bālānāṃ vālapaṃḍitānāṃ ca | . tṛtīyaṃ paṃḍitamaraṇaṃ yat kevalino anumriyate || 59|| ..

24

bṛhatpratyākhyānasaṃstarastavādhikāra 2| 33 mithyādarśanaraktā sanidānā kṛṣṇaleśyāmāgāḍhāḥ | iha ye niyaṃte jīvāḥ teṣāṃ punaḥ durlabhā bodhiḥ || 69 ||
artha-jo jīva atattvārthaśraddhānarūpa mithyādarśanameṃ līna haiṃ, āgāmī ākāṃkṣārūpa nidāna sahita haiṃ aura anaṃtānubaṃdhī kaṣāyase raṃjita yogakī pravṛttirūpa kṛṣṇaleśyākara sahita krūra pariṇāmī haiṃ aise jīva maraṇa karate haiṃ unake bodhi arthāt samyaktvasahita śubha pariṇāma honā durlabha hai || 69|| ___ āge anvayakara bodhiko kahate haiṃ;sammaiṃsaṇarattā aṇiyāṇā sukkalesamogāḍhā | iha je maraṃti jīvā tersi sulahā have bohī || 70 ||
samyagdarśanaraktā anidānāḥ śuklaleśyāmāgāḍhāḥ | iha ye niyaṃte jīvāḥ teṣāṃ sulabhā bhavet bodhiḥ || 70 ||
artha-jo jīva samyagdarśanameṃ līna haiṃ (tattvarucivāle haiṃ), isa loka paraloka saṃbaṃdhī bhogādikoṃkī icchā rahita haiṃ aura śuklaleśyārūpa śubha pariṇāmoṃ sahita haiṃ unake maraṇa samayameṃ bodhi honā sulabha hai || 70 || ___ āge saṃsārake kāraṇakā kharūpa kahate haiṃ;je puṇa gurupaḍiṇīyā bahumohā sasabalā kusīlā ya | asamāhiṇā maraṃte te hoṃti aṇaṃtasaṃsārā ||71|| ye punaḥ gurupratyanīkā bahumohāḥ saśabalāḥ kuśīlāḥ ca | asamādhinā mriyaṃte te bhavaṃti anaṃtasaṃsārāḥ || 71||
artha-jo ācāryādikoṃse pratikūla haiṃ, bahuta mohavāle haiṃ (rāgadveṣase pīḍa़ita haiṃ), khoṭe ācaraṇavāle haiṃ aura khoṭe śīla
3 mūlā.

mūlācāra(vratarakṣā ) vāle haiṃ aise jīva mithyātvasahita ārta raudra pariṇāmoṃkara maraṇa karate hue dīrgha saṃsārī hote haiṃ || 71 || __ āge alpasaṃsāravāle jīvoṃkā kharūpa batalāte haiṃjiṇavayaṇe aṇurattā guruvayaṇaṃ je karaṃti bhāveṇa | asabala asaṃkiliṭṭhā te hoṃti parittasaṃsārā ||72|| jinavacane anuraktāḥ guruvacanaṃ ye kurvati bhāvena | aśabalā asaṃkliṣṭāḥ te bhavaṃti parītasaṃsārāḥ || 72 ||
artha-jo puruṣa arhata bhāṣita pravacanameṃ acchītaraha bhakta haiṃ, ācāryādi guruoṃkī ājñāko bhaktise karate haiṃ maṃtra taṃtra śāstrapaṭhanakī ākāṃkṣāse kevala nahīṃ, mithyātvakara rahita haiṃ aura kleśa rahita śuddhapariṇāmavāle haiṃ ve alpasaṃsāravāle hote haiṃ ||72|| __ āge jinavacanameṃ anurāga na ho to kyā hotā hai usakā uttara kahate haiṃbālamaraṇāṇi bahuso bahuyāṇi akāmayāṇi maraṇāṇi marihaṃti te varāyā je jiṇavayaṇaṃ ṇa jāṇaṃti ||73|| bālamaraṇāni bahuśaḥ bahukāni akāmakāni maraṇāni | mariṣyati te varākā ye jinavacanaṃ na jānaṃti || 73 ||

bṛhatpratyākhyānasaṃstarastavādhikāra 2| 41 yathā niryāpakarahitā nāvo vararatnasupūrṇāḥ| pattanamāsannāḥ khalu pramādamūlā nibuḍaṃti || 88||

bṛhatpratyākhyānasaṃstarastavādhikāra 2| 43 saṃpūrṇa śrutaskaṃdha, śarīrabala manobala dhāraṇa karanevāle yatiyoṃse. bhī citavana nahīṃ kiyā jāsakatā arthāt na to arthakā vicāra banasakatā hai aura na pāṭha hī hosakatā hai || 92 || ___ āge kahate haiṃ ki aisā hai to kyā karanā;ekkami bidiyahmi pade saṃvego vīyarāgamaggammi | vajadi ṇaro abhikkhaṃ taṃ maraṇaṃte ṇa mottavvaṃ ||93 ||

artha-he kṣapaka ! jo sarvajñakathita āgamake 'namohadbhavaḥ' aise eka padameṃ tathā 'namaḥ siddhebhyaḥ' aisā dūsarā pada athavā arthapada graṃthapada pramāṇapada paṃcanamaskārapada athavā eka bījapadameṃ bhī jo saṃvega (harṣa) karatā hai vaha uttamagati pātā hai isa. liye kaṃṭhagata prāṇa honepara bhī padakā dhyāna nahīṃ choḍanā cāhiye || 93 ||

44
mūlācāra
| isaliye tujhako jinavacanakā āśraya
trayakā khāmī hotā hai nahīṃ choḍanā cāhiye ||
94 ||
āge maraṇake samaya pīḍā ho to kaunasī auṣadhi karanā use kahate haiṃ;
jiṇavayaṇamosahamiṇaṃ visayasuhavireyaṇaṃ amidabhūdaṃ | jaramaraṇa vāhiveyaṇa khayakaraṇaṃ savvadukkhāṇaṃ || 95 || jinavacanamauṣadhamidaṃ viṣayasukhavirecanaṃ amṛtabhūtaṃ | jarāmaraṇavyādhivedanānāṃ kṣayakaraṇaṃ sarvaduḥkhānām || 95 || artha - yaha jinavacana hī auṣadha hai | jo ki iṃdriya janita viṣayasukhoṃkā virecana karanevālī ( dūra karanevālī ) hai, amṛtakharūpa hai aura jarā maraṇa vyādhi vedanā ādi saba duḥkhoṃkā nāśa karanevālī hai | bhāvārtha jaise auṣadhi rogoṃko miṭā detī hai usī taraha jinavāṇī bhī janmamaraṇa ādi duḥkhoṃ ko miṭāke amara padako prāpta karadetī hai | isaliye amṛta auṣadhi jinavacana hī haiṃ || 95 ||
āge usa samaya śaraṇa kyā hai yaha batalāte haiṃ;ṇāṇaṃ saraṇaṃ meraṃ daṃsaṇasaraṇaṃ ca cariyasaraṇaṃ ca | tava saṃjamaṃ ca saraṇaṃ bhagavaṃ saraṇo mahāvīro || 96 || jñānaṃ śaraṇaṃ mama darśanaśaraṇaṃ ca cāritraśaraṇaṃ ca | tapaḥ saṃyamaśca śaraṇaṃ bhagavān śaraṇo mahāvīraḥ || 96 || artha – he kṣapaka tujhe aisī bhāvanā karanī cāhiye ki, mere yathārtha jñāna hī śaraṇa ( sahāyaka ) hai, praśama saṃvega anukaṃpā āstikyakī pragaṭatārūpa samyagdarśana hī śaraṇa hai, āsrava baṃdhaka

bṛhatpratyākhyānasaṃstarastavādhikāra 2| 45 nivṛttirūpa cāritra hī mere śaraṇa hai, bārahaprakāra tapa aura iṃdriya prāṇa saṃyama hī śaraṇa hai tathā anaṃta jñāna sukhādi sahita śrīmahāvīrakhāmī hitopadeśī hī śaraṇa haiṃ | inake sivāya anya kudevādikā śaraṇa mere nahīṃ hai || 96 ||
āge ārādhanāke phalako kahate haiṃārāhaṇa uvajutto kālaṃ kāūṇa suvihio samma | ukkassaṃ tiṇi bhave gaṃtūṇa ya lahai ṇivvāṇaṃ ||97||
ārādhanopayuktaḥ kālaṃ kṛtvā suvihitaḥ samyak | utkṛṣṭaṃ trīn bhavān gatvā ca labhate nirvāṇam || 97||
artha-samyagdarśana ādi cāra ārādhanākara upayukta huā atīcāra rahita ācaraṇavālā jo muni vaha acchītaraha maraṇakara utkṛṣṭa tīna bhava pākara nirvāṇa (mokṣa) ko pātā hai || 97 || __ aisā sunakara kṣapaka kāraṇapūrvaka pariṇāma karanekā abhilāṣī huā kahatā haisamaṇo metti ya paḍhamaṃ bidiyaṃ savvattha saṃjado metti | savvaṃ ca vossarāmi ya evaṃ bhaṇidaṃ samāseṇa || 98||
śramaṇo mama iti ca prathamaḥ dvitīyaḥ sarvatra saṃyato mameti | sarva ca vyutsṛjāmi ca etad bhaṇitaṃ samāsena || 98||
artha-kṣapaka vicāratā hai ki maiṃ prathama to śramaṇa arthāt samarasībhāvakara sahita hūṃ aura dūsare saba bhāvomeṃ saṃyamī hūṃ isakāraṇa saba ayogya bhāvoṃko choḍatā hūṃ | isataraha saṃkṣepase ālocanā kahā || 98||

46
mūlācāraāge phira dṛḍha pariṇāmoṃko dikhalāte haiṃ;laddhaṃ aladdhapuvaṃ jiṇavayaṇasubhāsidaṃ amidabhūdaṃ | gahido suggaimaggo ṇāhaṃ maraṇassa bīhemi || 99 ||
labdhamalabdhapūrva jinavacanasubhāṣitaṃ amṛtabhūtaṃ | gṛhītaḥ sugatimārgaḥ nāhaṃ maraṇādvibhemi || 99 ||
artha-kṣapaka vicāratā hai ki maiṃne pramāṇanayase aviruddha sukhakā kāraṇa, pūrva nahīṃ pāyā aise jinavacanako prāpta kiyā
aura mokṣamārga bhī grahaṇa kiyā | aba maiṃ maraṇase nahīṃ ḍaratā || bhāvārtha-jabataka ajñāna thā tabataka yathārthavarūpa nahīṃ jānā isaliye maraṇakā ḍara thā, aba jinavacanase yathārtha kharūpakā grahaṇa huā mokṣamārgameṃ pravṛtti huī taba maraṇakā bhaya jātā rahā ||99|| dhīraṇa vi maridavvaṃ ṇiddhīreṇavi avassa maridavvaṃ | jadi dohiṃ vimaridavvaṃ varaṃ hi dhīrattaṇeṇa maridabvaṃ 100
dhīreṇāpi martavyaṃ nidhairyeṇāpi avaśyaṃ martavyaṃ | yadi dvābhyāmapi martavyaṃ varaṃ hi dhīratvena martavyam ||10|| artha-kṣapakavicāratā hai ki dhīra (dṛḍhacitta) bhī maregā aura dhairyarahita bhī avaśya maregā | yadi donoṃ tarahase hī maranā hai to dhīra (kleśarahita) panese hī maranā śreṣṭha hai, kāyarapanese pāpabaṃdha viśeṣa karatā hai isaliye maraṇasamaya kāyara nahīṃ honā cāhiye || 100|| sīleṇavi maridavvaṃ ṇissīleṇavi avaśya maridavvaṃ | jai dohiṃvi mariyavvaṃ varaṃhu sīlattaṇeṇa mariyavvaṃ101

bṛhatpratyākhyānasaṃstarastavādhikāra 2| 47 śīlenāpi martavyaṃ niḥśīlenāpi avaśyaṃ martavyam | yadi dvābhyāmapi martavyaṃ varaṃ hi śīlatvena martavyam ||101||
artha-jo śīla (vratakī rakṣā) vāle haiṃ ve bhī mareṃge aura jo bhūkhapyāsa ādikī pīḍa़āse maraṇa honeke bhayase vrata śīla choḍa़ dete haiṃ ve bhī kāla ānepara avaśya mareṃge | yadi donoṃ taraha se hī maranā hai to śīlasahita hī maranā acchā hai | vrataśīla choḍa denese pāpabaṃdha adhika hogā maranā to paḍa़egā hī || 101 || ___ isaliye śīlasahita hī maranā śreṣṭha hai aisā kahate haiṃ;cirausidabaṃbhayārī papphoḍedūṇa sesayaṃ kammaṃ | aṇuputvīya visuddho suddho siddhiṃ gadi jādi ||102|| ciroṣitabrahmacārī prasphoṭya śeṣaṃ karma | ānupūā viśuddhaḥ śuddhaḥ siddhiṃ gatiṃ yāti || 102||
artha-jisane bahutakālataka brahmacaryavrata sevana kiyā hai aisā muni śeṣa jñānāvaraṇādi karmoṃkī nirjarākara kramase apūrva apūrva viśuddha pariṇāmoṃkara athavā guṇasthānake kramase asaṃkhyātaguṇaśreṇī nirjarākara karmakalaṃkase rahita huā kevalajñānādi śuddha bhāvoṃkara yukta hoke paramasthāna mokṣako prāpta hotā hai| aise ārādhanākā upāya jānanā || 102 || __ āge ārādhakakā kharūpa kahate haiṃṇimmamo ṇirahaṃkāro ṇikasāo jidiṃdio dhīro| aṇidāṇo diṭhisaṃpaṇṇo marato ārāhao hoi||103|| nirmamaḥ nirahaṃkāraḥ niṣkaṣāyaḥ jiteṃdriyaḥ dhīraḥ | anidānaḥ dṛṣṭisaṃpannaḥ mriyamāṇa ārādhako bhavati||103||

mūlācāra
artha - jo maraṇakaranevālā aisā ho - cetana acetana paravastu meṃ mamatā ( moha) nahīṃ ho, abhimāna rahita ho, krodhādikaṣāya rahita ho, jiteṃdriya ho arthāt viṣayasukhoṃse udāsīna tathā atīṃdriyasukhameṃ līna ho, parākrama sahita ho, śithila na ho, bhogoṃkī vāṃchākara rahita ho aura samyagdarśanako acchī taraha prāptahuā ho | aisā jīva ārādhaka hosakatā hai || 103 || āge isī bāta ko samarthana karate haiṃ; - ṇikkasāyarasa daṃtassa sūrassa vavasāiṇo | saṃsārabhayabhīdassa paccakkhāṇaṃ suhaṃ have || 104 || niṣkaṣāyasya dāṃtasya śūrasya vyavasāyinaḥ | saṃsārabhayabhītasya pratyākhyānaṃ sukhaṃ bhavet || 104 || artha — aise munirāja ke ārādhanā sukhakā nimitta hai - jo ki kaṣāya rahita ho, iṃdriyoṃko vaśa karanevālā ho, śūra ho kāyara na ho, cāritrameṃ udyamī - līna ho aura saṃsārake bhayase ḍaratā ho caturgatike duḥkhoṃke kharūpako jānatā ho | aisā maraṇa karane - vālā ārādhanākā ārādhaka hosakatā hai || 104 ||
48
aba kathanako saṃkocate hue ārādhanākā phala kahate haiṃ; - edaṃ pañcakkhāṇaṃ jo kāhadi maraṇadesayālammi | dhīro amūḍhasaṇṇo so gacchai uttamaṃ ṭhāṇaṃ || 105 || etat pratyākhyānaṃ yaḥ kuryāt maraṇadeśakāle |
dhīro amūḍhasaṃjñaḥ sa gacchati uttamaṃ sthānam || 105 || artha — jo muni maraṇake deśakālameṃ dhairya sahita, āhārādisaṃjñāmeṃ alubdha huā ( āhārādiko nahīṃ cāhatā huā ) isa

bṛhatpratyākhyānasaṃstarastavādhikāra 2| 19 pratyākhyānako karatā hai vaha mokṣasthānako prāpta hotā hai| ārādhanākā phala nirvāṇa hai yaha tātparya jānanā || 105||
āge aṃtamaṃgalapūrvaka prārthanā karate haiṃvīro jaramaraṇarivū vīro viṇṇāṇaṇāṇasaṃpaṇṇo| logassujjoyaparo jiṇavaracaṃdo disadu bodhiṃ ||10|| vīro jarāmaraṇaripuḥ vīro vijñānajñānasaṃpannaḥ | lokasya udyotakaro jinavaracaṃdro diśatu bodhim ||106||
artha-buḍhāpā tathā maraṇakā śatru (dūra karanevālā), viśeṣa lakṣmīkā denevālā, cāritra aura jñānakara sahita, bhavyajīvoṃke mithyātva aṃdhakārako miṭāke jñānarūpa prakāśakā karanevālā aura sāmānya kevaliyoṃmeṃ pradhāna caṃdramāke samāna ānaṃda karanevālā aisā mahāvīra prabhu cauvīsavāṃ tīrthaṃkara hameṃ samādhikī prāpti karāve | isa prakāra aṃtamaṃgalakara kṣapakako samādhikī prāptike kāraṇa mahāvīra svāmīkā smaraṇa dikhalāyā || 106|| __ āge nidāna nahīṃ karanā aura aisā bhāva karanā yaha kahate haiṃjā gadī arihaṃtāṇaṃ ṇiḍhidaṭṭhāṇa jā gadī| jā gadī vīdamohāṇaṃ sā me bhavadu sassadā ||107|| yā gatiḥ arhatāṃ niṣṭhitārthānāṃ yā gatiḥ| yā gatiḥ vītamohānāṃ sā me bhavatu śaśvat || 107|| artha-ācārya kahate haiṃ ki maiṃ aisī yācanā karatā hūṃ ki jo gati ahaṃtoṃkī hai, jo kṛtakṛtya siddha parameṣṭhiyoṃkī hai aura jo gati kṣīṇakaṣāya chadmastha ( alpajñānī) vītarāgoṃkī hai vahī
4 mūlā.

gati hameśā merī bhī hove ( rahe )| maiṃ dūsarī koī abhilāṣā va yācanā nahīṃ karatā | bhogakī abhilāṣākā nāma nidāna hai isaliye yahāṃ nidāna nahīṃ huā || 107 || isataraha adhikāra samāpta huā| isaprakāra ācāryaśrīvaṭṭakeriviracita mūlācārakī bhāṣāṭīkāmeṃ
bṛhatpratyākhyānasaṃstarastava adhikāra samāpta huā || 2 ||
.. saṃkṣepapratyākhyānādhikāra ||3||
āge akasmāt siṃhādike nimittase maraṇa ājāya to kyā karanā usake liye yaha saṃkṣepa pratyākhyāna adhikāra kahate haiṃ usameṃ bhī pahale maṃgalācaraṇa karate haiṃ;esa karemi paṇāmaṃ jiṇavaravasahassa vaḍmāṇassa| sesāṇaṃ ca jiṇāṇaṃ sagaṇagaṇadharāṇaṃ ca savvesiṃ 108
eṣaḥ karomi praṇāmaṃ jinavaravṛṣabhasya vardhamānasya | śeṣāṇāṃ ca jinānāṃ sagaṇagaṇadharāṇāṃ ca sarveṣām ||108|| artha-yaha maiṃ svasaṃvedana pratyakṣa vaṭṭakerācārya munirājoṃmeṃ śreṣṭha śrīmahāvīrasvāmīko, tathā yati muni ṛṣi anagāra aise cāra prakārake saṃghasahita gautamakhāmīko ādilekara saba gaṇadharoṃko aura śeṣa vṛṣabhādi pārśvanātha tīrthaṃkaroṃko ādilekara anya kevaliyoṃko namaskāra karatā hūṃ || bhāvārtha-saba paṃca parameṣṭhiyoṃko namaskāra karatā hūṃ || 108 ||

saṃkṣepapratyākhyānādhikāra 3 | āge saṃkṣepa pratyākhyāna karanekā krama batalāte haiṃ;-. savvaṃ pāṇāraṃbhaṃ paccakkhāmi alīyavayaṇaṃ ca | savvamadattādāṇaṃ mehuṇṇa pariggahaṃ ceva || 109 || sarva prāṇāraṃbhaṃ pratyākhyāmi alīkavacanaṃ ca | sarvamadattādānaṃ maithunaṃ parigrahaṃ caiva || 109 ||
artha-saṃkṣepatara pratyākhyāna karanevālā aise pratijñā karatā hai ki pahale to maiṃ saba hiṃsākā, jhūṭha bolanekā, corīkā; maithunakā tathā saba ābhyaṃtara bāhya parigrahakā pratyākhyāna (tyāga) karatā hūṃ | bhāvārtha-prathama to mahāvratoṃkī śuddhi karanī cāhiye || 109||
āge sāmāyikabatake svarūpakā varṇana karate haiṃ;sammaṃ me savvabhūdesu veraṃ majjhaṃ ṇa keṇavi | āsāe vosarittāṇaṃ samādhi paḍivajai||110|| sāmyaṃ me sarvabhūteṣu vairaṃ mama na kenāpi | āśāḥ vyutsṛjya samādhi pratipadye ||110 ||
artha—mere saba jīvoṃmeṃ samabhāva haiṃ, merā kisīke sātha vaira nahīṃ hai| isaliye maiṃ saba ākāṃkṣāoṃko choḍa़ samādhi (śuddha) pariṇāmako prāpta hotā hūṃ || bhāvārtha-saba jīvoṃmeṃ samabhāva rakhanā, vairabhāva kisīke ūpara na rakhanā, saba āśāoṃko choḍa़nā aura samādhibhāvako prāpta honā-isīkā nāma sāmāyika hai || 110 || __ āge pariṇāma śuddhike liye phira bhī kahate haiṃ;savvaṃ āhāravihiṃ saṇṇāo āsae kasāe ya |

52
mūlācārasavvaṃ ceya mamattiṃ jahāmi savvaṃ khamāvemi || 111|| sarva āhāravidhiṃ saṃjñā āśāḥ kaṣāyāśca | sarva caiva mamatvaṃ tyajāmi sarva kṣamayāmi || 111 ||
artha-maiṃ saba annapānādi āhārakī vidhiko, āhārādivāṃchāoṃko, isaloka paralokakī saba vāṃchāoṃko, krodha ādi kaṣāyoṃko, aura saba cetana acetana bāhyaparigrahameṃ mamatāko choḍatā hūṃ | isataraha pariṇāmoṃko śuddha karanā cāhiye ||111|| edamhi desayāle uvakkamo jīvidassa jadi majjhaṃ | edaṃ pacakkhāṇaṃ ṇitthipaṇe pāraṇā hojaṃ || 112 ||
etasmin deśakāle upakramo jīvitasya yadi mama | etat pratyākhyānaṃ nistīrṇe pāraṇā bhavet || 112 ||
artha-jīvitameṃ saṃdeha honekī avasthāmeṃ aisā vicāra kare ki isa deśameṃ isa kāla meṃ merā jīnekā sadbhāva ( astitva ) rahegā to aisā tyāga hai ki jabataka upasarga rahegā tabataka āhārādikā tyāga hai upasarga dūra honeke vāda yadi jīvita rahā to phira pāraṇā ( bhojana ) karūṃgā || 112 || __ jahāṃ niścaya hojāya ki isa upasargādimeṃ maiṃ nahīṃ jīsakū~gā vahāṃ aisā tyāga kare;savvaṃ āhāravihiṃ paccakkhāmī ya pāṇayaṃ vaja | uvahiṃ ca vosarāmiya duvihaṃ tiviheṇa sāvajaṃ||113||
sarva āhāravidhi pratyākhyāmi ca pānakaṃ varjayitvā | upadhiṃ ca vyutsṛjāmi dvividhaṃ trividhena sāvadyam ||113|| artha-maiṃ jalako choḍa़ saba (tīna) tarahake āhāroṃko tyāgatā

saṃkṣepapratyākhyānādhikāra 3 | hūṃ | bāhya ābhyaṃtara do prakārake parigrahako tathā mana vacana kāyakī pāpakriyāoṃko choḍatā hūṃ || 113 ||
āge uttamārtha tyāgako kahate haiṃjo koi majjha uvadhī sambhaṃtarabāhiro ya have | āhāraṃ ca sarīraṃ jāvājīvaṃ ya vosare ||114|| yaḥ kaścit mama upadhiḥ sābhyaṃtarabāhyaśca bhavet || āhāraṃ ca śarīraṃ yāvajjīvaṃ ca vyutsṛjāmi || 114 ||
artha-jo kucha mere ābhyaṃtara bāhya parigraha hai use tathā cāroṃ prakārake āhāroṃko aura apane śarīrako jabataka jīvana hai tabataka choḍa़tā hūṃ | yahī uttamārtha tyāga hai || 114 ||
āge āgamakī mahimā dekhakara jisako harṣa huā hai aisā kṣapaka isaprakāra namaskāra karatā hai;jamhiya līṇā jīvā taraṃti saṃsārasāyaramaṇaṃtaṃ | taṃ savvajīvasaraṇaṃ ṇaṃdau jiṇasāsaṇaṃ suiraṃ ||115||
yasmin līnā jīvāḥ taraṃti saṃsārasāgaraṃ anaṃtaṃ | tat sarvajīvaśaraṇaṃ naṃdatu jinaśāsanaṃ suciraṃ || 115 ||
artha-jisa jinaśāstrameṃ līna hue jīva apāra paṃcaparāvartanarūpasaṃsāra-samudrako tara jāte haiṃ aisā saba jīvoṃ kā sahāyaka kevalīśrutakevalīkathita āgama sabakāla vṛddhiko prāpta hovo || bhāvārtha-jisake anuṣṭhānase bhoga aura mukti mile vahī namaskāra karane yogya hotā hai || 115 || ___ āge ārādhanāke phalake liye kahate haiṃjā gadī arihaṃtāṇaṃ ṇihiṭṭhāṇa jā gadī|

54
mūlācārajā gadī vīdamohāṇaṃ sāme bhavadu savvadā || 116 || yā gatiḥ arhatāṃ niṣṭhitārthānāṃ yā gatiḥ | yā gatiḥ vītamohānāṃ sā me bhavatu sarvadā||116 || artha-jo arahaṃtoṃkī gati hai, jo siddhoṃkī gati hai, jo vītarāgachadmasthoṃkī gati hai vahī gati sarvadā ( hameśā ) merī bhī ho | yahī ārādhanākā phala cāhatā hūṃ anya nahīṃ || 116 || __āge uttamārtha tyāgakā phala kahate haiṃ;egaṃ paṃḍiyamaraṇaṃ chiṃdadi jādīsadāṇi bahugāṇi | taṃ maraṇaṃ maridavvaṃ jeṇa madaṃ summadaṃ hodi || 117 ||
ekaṃ paṃḍitamaraṇaṃ chinatti jātiśatāni bahūni | tanmaraṇena martavyaṃ yena mṛtaṃ sumṛtaṃ bhavati || 117||
artha-eka bhī paṃḍitamaraṇa saikaḍoṃ janmoṃkā chedanevālā hai, isaliye aisā maraṇa karanā cāhiye jisase ki maranā acchā maraṇa kahalāve arthāt phira janma nahīṃ dhāraṇa karanā paḍe ||117|| - āge maraṇakālameṃ samādhidhāraṇakā phala kahate haiṃ;egamhiya bhavagahaṇe samāhimaraṇaṃ lahija jadi jīvo| sattabhavaggahaṇe ṇivvāṇamaṇuttaraṃ lahadi || 198|| ekasin bhavagrahaṇe samādhimaraṇaṃ labhate yadi jīvaḥ | saptāṣṭabhavagrahaṇe nirvāṇamanuttaraṃ labhate || 118 ||
artha-jo yaha jīva eka hī paryāyameṃ saṃnyāsa maraṇako prāpta ho jāya to sāta āṭha paryāyeṃ vīta jānepara avaśya mokṣako pātā hai || 118 || yahāṃ bhāvaliṃgīkeliye hī kahāgayā hai|
āge śarīrake honese hī janmamaraṇādi duḥkha hote haiṃ

ityādika jisa sthānameṃ rahanese vaḍhe utkṛṣṭa hoṃ usa kṣetrameṃ rahanā vaha kṣetropasaṃpat hai || 141 ||
āge mārgopasaṃpatko kahate haiṃ;-. pāhuṇavatthavāṇaṃ aṇṇoṇṇāgamaṇagamaṇasuhapucchā| upasaṃpadā ya magge saṃjamatavaṇāṇajogajuttāṇaṃ 142 pādoṣṇavāstavyānāmanyonyāgamanagamanasukhapraśnaḥ | upasaṃpat ca mārge saṃyamatapojñānayogayuktānām || 142 ||
artha-anya saṃghake āye hue muni tathā apane sthānameṃ rahanevāle muniyoṃse āpasameṃ āne jāneke viṣayameṃ kuśalakā pūchanā ki ' ānaṃdase āye va sukhase pahuṃce ' isataraha pūchanā vaha saṃyamatapajñānayoga-guṇoṃkara sahita munirājoṃke mārgopasaṃpat hotā hai || 142 ||

sukhaduḥkhayoḥ upacāro vasatiāhārabheṣajādibhiḥ | yuṣmākaṃ ahaṃ iti vacanaṃ sukhaduḥkhopasaṃpat jñeyā ||143||
artha—sukha duḥkha yukta puruṣoṃko vasatikā āhāra auṣadhi ādikara upakāra ( sukhī ) karanā arthāt śiṣyādikā lābha honepara kamaṃḍalu ādi denā vyādhikara pīḍita hue ko sukharūpa sonekā sthāna baiṭhanekā sthāna batānā, auṣadha annapāna milanekā prakāra batānā aṃga malanā tathā maiṃ āpakā hūṃ āpa ājñā kareṃ

artha-jo vaha āgaṃtuka muni caraṇakaraṇase aśuddha ho devavaṃdanākara ayogya ho to prāyaścitta śāstrako dekhakara cheda tathā upasthāpanā karanā | jo vaha chedopasthāpanā khīkāra na kare to use choḍa़ de | aura jo ayogyako bhī mohase grahaṇa kare use prāyaścitta na de to vaha ācārya bhī prāyaścittake yogya hai ||168|| ___ usake bāda kyā karanā cāhiye yaha kahate haiṃevaṃ vidhiṇuvavaṇṇo evaṃ vidhiṇeva sovi saṃgahido| suttatthaṃ sikkhaṃto evaṃ kujjā payatteṇa || 169||

samācārādhikāra 4 | vidhānakara hī ācāryoṃse ācaraṇakī śuddhatā kare aura ācāryoṃse yatnācārapūrvaka sūtrārtha sīkhe || 169||
āge yatnācāra kaise kare yaha kahate haiṃpaḍilehiūṇa sammaṃ davvaṃ khettaṃ ca kālabhāve ya | viṇavovayārajutteṇajjhedavvaṃ payatteṇa || 170|| pratyālekhya samyak dravyaṃ kṣetraṃ ca kālabhāvau ca | vinayopacārayuktenādhyetavyaṃ prayatnena || 170 ||

samācārādhikāra 4 |
paṃca ṣaṭ sapta hastān sūri adhyāpakaṃ ca sādhūśva | parihatya āyoḥ gavāsanenaiva vaṃdate || 195 ||

artha-isaprakāra maiṃne saṃkṣepase bahuta bhedavālā samācāra arthāt āgamaprasiddha anuṣṭhāna barṇana kiyā hai, isakā vistārakathana buddhimānoṃko vistārita karanā cāhiye || 197 || isaprakāra ācāryaśrīvaṭṭakeriviracita mūlācārakī hiṃdībhāṣāṭīkāmeṃ samācāroṃko kahanevālā cauthā samācārādhikāra

āge nihnavakā svarūpa kahate haiṃkulavayasīlavihaṇe muttatthaṃ sammagāgamittāṇaṃ | kulavayasīlamahalle ṇiṇhavadoso du jappaṃto ||284 ||

āge vinayakaranekā phala dikhalāte haiṃ;viṇaeṇa sudamadhīdaṃ jadivi pamādeṇa hodi vissaridaṃ | tamuvaṭṭhādi parabhave kevalaṇāṇaṃ ca āvahādi || 286 || vinayena śrutamadhītaṃ yadyapi pramādena bhavati vismṛtaṃ | tadupatiṣṭhate parabhave kevalajñānaṃ ca āvahati || 286 ||

paṃcācārādhikāra 5|
121 artha-hāsyase, bhayase, krodhase, lobhase mana vacana kāyakara kisī samayameṃ bhī viśvāsaghātaka dūsareko pīḍā karanevālā jhūṭha vacana na bole | vaha satyavrata hai || 290 || gāme ṇagareraṇe thūla sacittaṃ bahu sapaḍivakkhaṃ | tiviheṇa vajivvaṃ adiṇṇagahaṇaṃ ca taṇṇicaṃ ||291 grāme nagare'raṇye sthūlaṃ sacittaṃ bahu sapratipakṣaṃ | trividhena varjitavyaṃ adattagrahaṇaṃ ca tannityaṃ || 291 ||

mūlācāragrāmaṃ nagaraṃ araṇyaṃ sthūlaṃ sacittaṃ bahu sapratipakṣaṃ | adhyātma bahiḥsthaṃ trividhena parigrahaṃ varjayet || 293 ||

paṃcācārādhikāra 5|
123
tesiṃ paṃcapi ya vayāṇamāvajjaṇaṃ ca saṃkā vā | ādavivattī a have rādī bhattappasaṃgeṇa || 296 || teṣāṃ paṃcānāmapi ca vratānāmāvarjanaṃ ca śaṃkā vā | ātmavipattiśca bhaveta rātribhaktaprasaṃgena || 296 || artha — una muniyoṃke rātribhojana ke liye gamana karane se pāṃca vratoṃkā bhaṃga athavā malinatā, cora ādikī śaṃkā aura kotavāla ādise baṃdhane ādikī vipatti apane ūpara āpaḍa़tī hai| isaliye rātribhojanakā tyāga avaśya karanā || 296 ||
āge āṭha pravacanamātāoṃse āṭha bheda cāritrake hote haiṃ;paṇidhāṇajogajutto paṃcasu samidīsu tīsu guttīsu | esa carittācāro aṭṭhavidho hoi ṇāyavvo || 297 ||
praṇidhānayogayukta paṃcasu samitiṣu triṣu guptiṣu | eṣa caritrācāraḥ aṣṭavidho bhavati jñātavyaḥ || 297 || artha — pariṇāmake saṃyogase pāṃca samiti tīna guptiyoṃ meṃ nyāyarūpa pravṛtti vaha āṭha bhedavālā cāritrācāra hai aisā jānanā || 297 ||
paṇidhāpi yaduvihaṃ pasattha taha apasatthaṃ ca | samidīsu ya guttī ya satthaṃ sesamappasatthaṃ tu 298 praṇidhānamapi ca dvividhaṃ praśastaṃ tathā apraśastaṃ ca | samitiṣu ca guptiṣu ca śastaṃ śeṣamapraśastaṃ tu || 298 ||

mūlācārasaharasarūvagaṃdhe phāse ya maṇohare ya idare ya| jaṃ rāgadosagamaṇaṃ paṃcavihaṃ hoi paṇidhāṇaṃ || 299 ||

paṃcācārādhikāra 5| maragujjovupaogālaṃbaṇasuddhīhiṃ iriyado muṇiṇo| suttāṇuvīci bhaṇiyā iriyāsamidī pavayaṇammi 302 mārgodyotopayogālaṃbanaśuddhibhiḥ īyato muneḥ| sūtrānuvīcyā bhaṇitā īryāsamitiḥ pravacane || 302|| artha-mārga, netra sūryakā prakāśa, jñānādimeṃ yatna, devatā ādi ālaṃbana-inakī śuddhatāse tathā prāyaścittādi sūtroṃke anusārase gamana karate munike īryāsamiti hotī hai aisā āgamameṃ kahā hai || 302 || iriyāvahapaḍivaṇṇeṇavalogaṃteṇa hodi gaṃtavvaṃ | purado jugappamāṇaṃ sayāppamatteṇa satteṇa || 303 || īryāpathapratipannenāvalokayatā bhavati gaṃtavyaṃ | purataḥ yugapramāṇaṃ sadā apramattena satā || 303||

paṃcācārādhikāra 5|
127 saṃbhāvanāvyavahāre bhāve aupamyasatye ca ||308||
artha-satyavacanake dasa bheda haiṃ-janapadasatya, saṃmatasatya, sthāpanāsatya, nāmasatya, rūpasatya, pratītyasatya, saṃbhāvanāsatya, vyavahārasatya, bhāvasatya, upamāsatya || 308 || jaṇapadasacaṃ jadha odaṇādi rucide ya savvabhāsāe| bahujaṇasammadamavi hodi jaṃ tu loe tahā devī 309
janapadasatyaṃ yathā odanādiraucitye ca sarvabhāṣayā | bahujanasammatamapi bhavati yattu loke tathā devī || 309||
artha-deśasatya vaha hai ki jo saba bhāṣāoṃse bhātake nāma jude 2 bole jāte haiṃ jaise corū kūla bhakta | aura bahutajanoṃkara mānā gayā jo nāma vaha saṃmatasatya hai jaise lokameṃ rājākī strīko devī kahanā || 309|| ṭhavaṇā ṭhavidaṃ jaha devadādi ṇāmaṃ ca devadattādi | ukkaḍadarotti vaṇṇe rūve seo jadha balāyā ||310|| sthāpanā sthāpitaṃ yathā devatādi nāma ca devadattādi | utkaṭatara iti varṇena rūpe śvetā yathā balākā || 310||
artha-jo ahaṃta ādikī pāṣāṇa. ādimeṃ sthāpanā vaha sthāpanāsatya hai | jo guṇakī apekṣā na rakhakara vyavahārake liye devadatta ādi nāma rakhanā vaha nāma satya hai aura jo rūpake bahutapanese kahanā ki bagulāoṃkī paṃkti sapheda hotī hai yaha rūmasatya hai || 310 || aṇṇaṃ apecchasiddhaṃ paḍuccasatyaṃ jahā havadi digdhaṃ | vavahāreṇa ya sacaṃ rajjhadi kūro jahā loe || 311||

128
mūlācāraanyadapekṣyasiddhaṃ pratītyasatyaṃ yathā bhavati dīrgha | vyavahāreṇa ca satyaṃ radhyate krūro yathā loke ||311 ||
artha-anyakī apekṣāse jo kahā jāya vaha pratītyasatya hai jaise yaha dīrgha (baḍā ) hai yahāṃ hūkhakī apekṣāse hai | jo lokameṃ bhāta pakatā hai aisā vacana kahā jātā hai vaha vyavahārasatya hai 311 saṃbhāvaṇā ya sacaṃ jadi ṇāmecchenja eva kujaṃti | jadi sako icchenjo jaṃbūdīvaṃ hi pallatthe || 312||

artha-jaisī icchā rakhe vaisā hī karasake vaha saṃbhāvanāsatya hai jaise iṃdra icchā kare to jaṃbūdvīpako palaṭā sakatā hai || 312|| hiṃsādidosavijudaṃ saccamakappiyavi bhāvado bhāvaṃ | ovammeṇa dusatyaṃ jāṇasu palidovamādīyā ||313|| hiṃsādidoṣaviyutaṃ satyamakalpitamapi bhāvato bhāvaṃ |
aupamyena tu satyaṃ jānīhi palyopamādikaṃ || 313 || artha-jo hiṃsādi doṣa rahita ayogya vacana bhī ho vaha bhāvasatya hai jaise kisīne pūchā ki cora dekhā usane kahā ki nahīṃ dekhā | jo upamā sahita ho vaha vacana upamāsatya hai jaise palyopama sāgaropama ādi kahanā || 313 ||
aba asatyādivacanako kahate haiṃ;tavivarīdaṃ mosaṃ taṃ ubhayaṃ jattha saccamosaṃ taṃ | tavivarīdā bhāsā asaccamosā havadi diṭṭhā ||314||
tadviparītaṃ mṛṣā tadubhayaṃ yatra satyamṛṣā tat |

paṃcācārādhikāra 5|
122
tadviparītā bhāṣā asatyamṛṣā bhavati dṛṣṭā ||314 ||
artha-dasa satyoṃse ulaṭā jo vacana vaha asatyavacana hai, jahāṃ donoṃ haiṃ vaha satyamṛṣā hai aura jo isase viparīta hai vaha asatyamṛṣā bhāṣā hai || 314 ||
aba asatyamṛṣāvacanake bheda kahate haiṃāmaṃtaṇi āṇavaṇī jāyaṇi saṃpucchaṇī ya paṇṇavaṇī| pañcakkhāṇī bhāsā chaṭṭhī icchāṇulomā ya || 315|| saṃsayavayaṇī ya tahā asacamosā ya aṭṭhamī bhāsā | ṇavamī aṇakkharagayā asaccamosā havadi dihā 316
āmaṃtraṇī ājñāpanī yācanī saṃpṛcchanī ca prajñāpanī | pratyākhyānī bhāṣā ṣaṣṭhī icchānulomā ca || 315 || saṃśayavacanī ca tathā asatyamṛṣā ca aṣṭamī bhāṣā | navamī anakṣaragatā asatyamṛṣā bhavati dṛṣṭā || 316 ||
artha-he devadatta aisā bolakara saṃmukhakaranā vaha āmaṃtraṇī bhāṣā, ājñā karanerūpa ājñāpanī, yācanībhāṣā, pūchanerūpa pṛcchanī bhāṣā, jatalānerūpa prajñāpanī bhāṣā, tyāga lenerūpa pratyākhyānī bhāṣā, icchāke anukūla bolanerūpa icchānulomā chaṭhī bhāṣā | saṃśayarūpa arthako kahanevālī saṃśayavacanī bhāṣā, bhaiṃsa ādikā śabda kharūpa āṭhamī asatyamṛṣā hai | aura anakṣarī divyadhvanirūpa vāṇī vaha naumī anakṣaragatā asatyamṛṣā kahī hai| ina bhāṣāoṃmeṃ viśeṣakā jānanā na honese satya bhī nahīṃ kahasakate aura sāmānya jñāna honese asatya bhī nahīṃ kahasakate, isaliye ye nau asatyamṛṣā bhāṣā kahalātī haiṃ || 315 || 16 ||

133 artha-usī kahe hue kramase pratiṣṭhāpanā samiti bhī varṇana kī gaī hai usīkramase tyāgane yogya malamūtrādiko ukta sthaṃḍila sthānameṃ nikṣepaṇa kare | usīke pratiṣṭhāpanā samiti śuddha hotī hai || 325|| edāhiṃ sayā jutto samidīhiṃ mahiṃ viharamāṇovi | hiṃsādīhiṃ ṇa lippai jīvaṇikāāule sāhū ||326||

149 artha-ātāpanādi uttara guṇoṃmeṃ utsāha, śramako nirākulatāse sahanā, prīti aura chaha āvaśyakoṃmeṃse kamatī baḍhatī nahīṃ karanā || 370 || bhattī tavodhiyamhi ya tavamhi ahīlaṇā ya sesāṇaṃ| eso tavamhi viṇao jahuttacarittasāhussa || 371|| bhaktiḥ tapodhike ca tapasi ahelanāṃ ca śeṣāṇāṃ | eṣa tapasi vinayaḥ yathoktacāritrasādhoḥ || 371 ||

artha-guru ādike pīche khaḍa़e rahanā, pīche gamana karanā, nīce baiṭhanā, nīce sonā, guruoṃko āsana denā, pustaka ādi dharmopakaraṇa denā, prāsuka vasatikā vatādenā-ityādi kāyavinaya hai || 374 || paḍirūvakāyasaṃphāsaṇadā paḍirūpakālakiriyā ya | posaṇakaraṇaṃ saṃtharakaraṇaṃ uvakaraṇapaḍilihaṇaṃ || 375||
pratirūpakāyasaṃsparśanatā pratirūpakālakriyā ca | preṣyakaraṇaṃ saṃstarakaraṇaṃ upakaraṇaṃ pratilekhanaṃ || 375 ||
artha-balake anusāra śarīrakā sparśana mardana, kālake anusāra kriyā karanā arthāt uṣṇakālameṃ śītakriyā śītakālameṃ uṣṇakriyā, ājñāke anusāra karanā, saṃthārā karadenā, pustakādikā sodhadenā || 375|| iccevamādio jo uvayāro kīrade sarīreṇa | ..

paṃcācārādhikāra 5| eso kāiyaviṇao jahārihaṃ sādhuvaggassa || 376 ||

āge vācikavinayakā kharūpa kahate haiṃpūyāvayaṇaṃ hidabhāsaṇaṃ ca midabhāsaṇaṃ ca madhuraṃ ca | suttāṇuvīcivayaṇaṃ aṇijhuramakakasaṃ vayaṇaṃ || 377 || pūjāvacanaṃ hitabhāṣaṇaṃ ca mitabhāṣaṇaṃ ca madhuraṃ ca | sūtrānuvīcivacanaṃ aniṣṭuramakarkaśaṃ vacanaṃ || 377 ||
artha-ūṃce (pūjya ) vacanoṃse bolanā, hitarūpa bolanā, thoḍa़ā bolanā, miṣṭa bolanā, āgamake anusāra bolanā, kaṭhoratā rahita vacana bolanā, || 377 || uvasaṃtavayaṇamagihatthavayaṇamakiriyamahīlaṇaṃ vayaṇaṃ | eso vāiyaviṇao jahārihaṃ hodi kādavvo ||378|| upaśāṃtavacanaṃ agṛhasthavacanaṃ akriyamahīlanaṃ vacanaṃ | eṣa vācikavinayaḥ yathārha bhavati kartavyaḥ || 378 ||
artha-krodhādirahita vacana, baṃdhana ādi rahita vacana, asi. ādi kriyā rahita vacana, abhimānarahita vacana, bolanā-vaha vācikavinaya hai use yathāyogya karanā cāhiye || 378 ||
āge mānasika vinayako kahate haiṃpāpavisotiapariṇāmavajaṇaṃ piyahide ya pariṇāmo| ṇādavo saṃkheveṇeso māṇasio viṇao.|| 379||

paṃcācārādhikāra 5|
157 artha-paḍhe hue graṃthakā pāṭhakaranā, śāstrakā vyākhyāna karanā, śāstroṃ ke arthako dūsarese pūchanā, vāraṃvāra śāstrakā manana karanā, tresaṭha śalākā puruṣoṃkā caritra paḍhanā ye pāṃca prakārakā khādhyāya hai | ise munidevavaṃdanā maṃgala sahita karanā cāhiye || 393 || ahaṃ ca ruddasahiyaṃ doṇṇivi jhāṇāṇi appasatthāṇi | dhammaṃ sukaṃ ca duve pasatthajhāṇāṇi ṇeyāṇi || 394 ||
ārta ca raudrasahitaṃ dve api dhyāne apraśaste | dharma śuklaṃ ca dve praśastadhyāne jñātavyāni || 394 ||
artha-ārtadhyāna raudradhyāna ye do dhyāna aśubha haiṃ narakādiduḥkhoṃko prāpta karāte haiṃ tathā dharmadhyāna śukladhyāna ye do dhyāna śubha haiṃ mokṣādike sukhoṃko prāpta karāte haiṃ | aisā jānanā cāhiye || 394 ||
āge ina cāroṃkā kharūpa kahate haiṃ;amaṇuṇṇajogaiṭṭaviogaparīsahaṇidāṇakaraṇesu | ajhaṃ kasāyasahiyaṃ jhāṇaṃ bhaṇidaṃ samāseṇa || 395 ||
amanojñayogaiṣṭaviyogaparīṣahanidānakaraṇeṣu | ārta kaṣāyasahitaṃ dhyānaṃ bhaṇitaṃ samāsena || 395 ||
artha-jvara śūla śatru ādi apriya vastukā saṃbaṃdha honā, putra putrī mātā śiṣya ādi priyavastukā vināśa honā, kṣudhā (bhūkha ) ādi pariṣahoṃkī bādhā honā, paralokasaṃbaṃdhī bhogoṃkī vāṃchā honā-inake honepara jo kaṣāyasahita manako kleśa honā vaha saṃkṣepase ārtadhyāna kahā gayā hai || 395 || teṇikamosasārakkhaṇesu tadha ceva chavihāraṃbhe|

paṃcācārādhikāra 5| dhyānakā citavanakara | usake ājñāvicaya apāyavicaya vipākavicaya saṃsthānavicaya aise cāra bheda haiṃ || 398 || paṃcatthikāyachajjīvaṇikāye kāladavvamaṇṇe ya| āṇāgejjhe bhāve āṇāvicayeṇa viciṇādi ||399|| paṃcāstikāyaṣaṭjīvanikāyān kāladravyamanyat ca | ājñāgrāhyān bhāvān ājñāvicayena vicinoti ||399 ||
artha-jīvādi paṃca astikāya, pṛthivīkāya ādi chaha jīvakāya, kāladravya, ye saba sarvajñakī ājñāpramāṇa grahaṇa karane yogya haiṃ isataraha ājñāmātrase śraddhāna karanā vicāranā vaha ājñāvicaya dharmadhyāna hai || 399 || kallāṇapāvagāo pāo viciṇodi jiṇamadamuvica | viciṇādi vā apāye jīvāṇa suhe ya asuhe ya 400 kalyāṇaprāpakān upāyān vicinoti jinamatamupetya | vicinoti vā apāyān jīvānāṃ śubhān ca aśubhān ca 400

artha-eka bhavameṃ prāpta tathā anekabhavoṃmeṃ prāpta jīvoṃke puṇyakarma pāpakarmoṃke phalako vicāranā tathā karmoṃkā udaya apakka

paṃcācārādhikāra 5| artha–upaśāṃtakaṣāyaguṇasthānavālā jīva pṛthaktvavitarkavīcāra nāmā śukladhyānako dhyātā hai aura kṣīṇakaṣāyaguṇasthānavālā ekatvavitarkavīcāra nāmā dūsare śukladhyāna kā citavana karatā hai||40|| muhumakiriyaṃ sajogī jhāyadi jhāṇaṃ ca tadiyasukaṃ tu| jaṃ kevalī ajogī jhāyadi jhāṇaṃ samucchiṇṇaṃ 405 sūkṣmakriyaṃ sayogī dhyāyati dhyānaṃ ca tṛtīyaśuklaṃ tu | yat kevalī ayogī dhyāyati dhyānaṃ samucchinnaṃ ||405||
artha-sūkṣmakāyakriyāpratipāti nāmaka tīsare śukladhyānako sayoga kevalī dhyāte haiṃ aura samucchinnakriya nāmake cauthe śukladhyānako ayogakevalī dhyāte haiṃ || 405 ||
āge vyutsargatapakā nirūpaṇa karate haiṃ;duviho ya viussago abbhaṃtara bāhiro muṇeyavvo| anbhaṃtara kohādī bāhira khettādiyaṃ davvaṃ || 406 || dvividhaśca vyutsargaḥ ābhyaṃtaro bāhyaḥ jñātavyaḥ | abhyaṃtaraḥ krodhādiḥ bāhyaḥ kṣetrādikaṃ dravyaṃ || 406 ||
artha-parigrahatyāgarūpa vyutsargatapa do prakārakā hai eka abhyaṃtara dūsarā bāhya | krodhādikā tyāga honā abhyaṃtara vyutsarga hai
aura kṣetrādi bāhyadravyakā tyāga vaha bāhya vyutsarga hai || 406 || micchattavedarāgā taheva hassādiyā ya chaddosā| cattāri taha kasāyā coddasa abbhaṃtarā gaṃthā ||407|| mithyātvavedarāgā tathaiva hāsyādikāśca padoṣāḥ| catvāraḥ tathā kaṣāyāḥ caturdaśa ābhyaṃtarā graṃthāḥ||407|| artha-mithyātva, tīna veda (strī ādi), rāga, hāsya ādi

paṃcācārādhikāra 5| saṃvara karatā hai mana ādi tīnaguptiyoṃkā bhī pālanevālā hotā hai aura ekāgracitta huā vinayakara saṃyukta hotā hai || 410|| siddhippāsāvadaṃsayassa karaṇaṃ caduvviho hodi | davve khette kāle bhāvevi ya āṇupuvvīe||411|| siddhiprāsādāvataṃsakasya karaṇaṃ caturvidhaṃ bhavati | dravyaṃ kṣetra kālaṃ bhāvamapi ca ānupūyā || 411||

āge maṃgalācaraṇapūrvaka piṃḍaśuddhi kahanekī pratijñā karate haiṃ:tiradṇapuruguṇasahide arahaṃte vididasayalasanbhāve | paṇamiya sirasā vocchaṃ samāsado piṃḍasuddhī du 420 triratnapuruguṇasahitān arhataḥ viditasakalasadbhāvān | praṇamya śirasā vakṣye samāsataḥ piṃḍaśuddhistu || 420 || artha — samyagdarśanāditīnaratnarūpī mahāna guṇoṃkara sahita saba padārthoṃ ke jānanevāle aise arahaṃtoṃko mastaka navākara maiṃ saṃkṣepase āhāraśuddhiko kahatā hūṃ || 420 ||
uggama uppādaṇa esaṇaṃ ca saṃjojaṇaṃ pamāṇaṃ ca | iṃgāladhūmakāraṇa aṭṭhavihā piṃḍasuddhī du || 421 || udgamaḥ utpādanaṃ eṣaṇaṃ ca saṃyojanaṃ pramāṇaṃ ca | aṃgāraṃ dhūmaḥ kāraṇaṃ aṣṭavidhā piṃḍaśuddhistu || 421 || artha – udgama utpādana aśana saṃyojana pramāṇa aṃgāra dhūma kāraṇa- ina āṭha doṣoṃkara rahita jo bhojana (āhāra) lenā vaha āṭha prakārakī piṃḍaśuddhi kahī hai || 421 || ādhākammuddesiya ajjhovajjñeya pūdi misse ya | ṭhavide bali pāhuḍide pādūkāre ya kī ya || 422 || pāmicche pariyahe abhihaḍamacchiṇṇa mālaārohe | acchijje aṇisaṭṭhe uggamadosā du solasime || 423 adhaḥ karma auddeśikaṃ adhyadhi pūtiḥ miśrava |

piṇḍaśuddhi-adhikāra 6| 169 auddeśika hai | athavā saṃkṣepase samaudeśikake kahe jānevāle cāra bheda haiṃ || 425 || jāvadiyaṃ uddeso pāsaṃḍotti ya have samuddeso| samaṇotti ya ādeso ṇiggaṃthotti ya have samādeso|| yāvān uddeśaḥ pāṣaṃḍa iti ca bhavet samuddeśaḥ | śramaṇa iti ca ādeśo nigraṃtha iti ca bhavet samādeśaḥ||
artha-jo koī āyegā sabako deṃge aise uddeśase kiyā anna yāvānuddeśa 1 hai, pākhaṃḍī anyaliṃgīke nimittase banā huā anna samuddeśa hai 2, tāpasa parivrājaka ādike banāyā bhojana ādeśa hai 3, nigraṃtha (digaṃbara ) sādhuoṃke nimitta banāyā gayā samādeśa doṣa sahita hai 4 || ye cāra auddeśikake bheda haiṃ ||426
āge adhyadhidoṣakā svarūpa kahate haiṃ;jalataṃdulapakkhevo dāṇaṭuṃ saṃjadāṇa sayapayaṇe| ajjhovojjhaṃ ṇeyaṃ ahavā pāgaṃ tu jāva roho vā || jalataṃdulaprakṣepo dānārtha saṃyatānāṃ svapacane| adhyadhi jñeyaṃ athavā pākaṃ tu yāvat rodho vā ||427 ||
artha-saṃyamī sādhuko ātā dekha unako deneke liye apane nimitta bhātakeliye cūlhepara rakhe hue jala aura cāṃvaloṃmeṃ jala aura cāṃvala milākara phira pakāve athavā jaba taka bhojana tayāra na ho tabataka dharma praśnake bahānese usa sādhuko roka rakhe vaha adhyadhidoṣa hai || 427 || .. . appāsueṇa missaṃ pāsuyadavvaṃ tu pūdikammaṃ taṃ | cullī ukkhali davvī bhāyaṇagaṃdhatti paṃcavihaṃ ||428||

170
mūlācāraaprāsukena miśraṃ prāsukadravyaṃ tu pūtikarma tat | cullī udūkhalaḥ darvī bhājanaṃ gaṃdha iti pañcavidhaṃ ||428||
artha-prāsuka āhārādika vastu sacittādivastuse miśrita ho vaha pūtidoṣa hai | prāsukadravya bhī pūtikarmase milā pūtikarma kahalātā hai usake pāṃca bheda haiṃ-cūli okhalī kaḍa़chī pakāneke bāsana gaṃdhayukta dravya | ina pāṃcoṃmeṃ saṃkalpa karanā ki cūli ādise pakā huā bhojana jabataka sādhuko na dedeṃ tabataka kisīko nahīṃ deṃge | ye hī pāṃca āraṃbha doṣa haiṃ || 428 || __ āge miśradoṣako kahate haiṃpāsaṃḍehiṃ ya saddhaṃ sāgārehiṃ ya jadaṇṇamuddisiyaṃ | dādumidi saṃjadāṇaṃ siddhaṃ missaṃ viyāṇāhi ||429|| pākhaṇḍaiḥ sārdha sāgāraiśca yadannaṃ uddiṣṭaṃ | dātumiti saṃyatānāṃ siddhaṃ miśraṃ vijānīhi || 429 ||

artha-jisa bāsanameṃ pakāyā thā usase dūsare bhājanameṃ pake bhojanako rakhakara apane gharameṃ tathā dūsareke gharameṃ jākara usa annako rakha de use sthāpita doṣa jānanā || 430 ||

piṇḍaśuddhi-adhikāra 6| 171 jakkhayaṇāgādīṇaṃ balisesaṃ sa balitti paṇṇattaṃ | saṃjadaāgamaṇa8 baliyamma vā baliṃ jāṇe || 431 ||

piṇḍaśuddhi-adhikāra 6| 181 artha-paḍhanemātrase jo maṃtra siddha ho vaha paṭhita siddha maṃtra hotā hai usa maṃtrakī āśā ( lobha ) dekara aura usakī mahimā kahakara jo sādhu āhāra grahaṇa karatā hai usake maṃtradoṣa hotā hai || 458 || āhāradāyagāṇaṃ vijāmaṃtehiṃ devadāṇaṃ tu | āhūya sādhidavvā vijāmaṃto have doso || 459 ||

artha-āhārake denevāle vyaṃtarādidevoṃko vidyā tathā maṃtrase bulākara sādhana kare vaha vidyāmaṃtra doṣa hai| athavā āhāra denevāle gṛhasthoṃke liye devatāko bulākara sādhanā vaha bhī vidyāmaṃtradoṣa hai || 459 || ṇettassaṃjaṇacuṇṇaṃ bhūsaṇacuṇṇaṃ ca gattasobhayaraṃ | cuṇṇaṃ teṇuppādo cuṇṇayadoso havadi eso|| 460|| netrayoraṃjanacūrṇa bhūṣaṇacūrṇa ca gātraśobhākaraṃ | cūrṇa tenotpādaḥ cūrṇadoṣo bhavati eṣaḥ || 460 ||
artha-netroṃkā aṃjana, bhūṣaṇa sāpha karanekā cūrṇa, śarīrakī śobhā baḍhāne vālā cūrṇa-ina cūrṇāṃkī vidhi batalākara āhāra le vahāṃ cūrṇadoṣa hotā hai || 460 || avasāṇaṃ vasiyaraṇaṃ saṃjojayaṇaṃ ca vippajuttāṇaṃ | bhaṇidaṃ tu mūlakammaṃ ede uppādaṇā dosā || 461 || avaśānāṃ vaśīkaraṇaṃ saṃyojanaṃ ca viprayuktānāṃ | bhaṇitaṃ tu mūlakarma ete utpādanā doṣāḥ || 461 ||

artha–sādhu dravya aura bhāva donoṃse prāsuka dravyakā bhojana kare| jisameṃse ekeṃdrī jīva nikala gaye vaha dravya prāsuka ( śuddha ) hai | aura jo prāsuka āhāra honepara bhī 'mereliye kiyā hai' aisā ciṃtana kare vaha bhāvase aśuddha jānanā | tathā ciṃtana nahīṃ karanā vaha bhāvaśuddha āhāra hai || 485 || jaha macchayāṇa payade madaṇudaye macchayā hi majati | ṇa hi maṃḍūgā evaṃ paramaṭṭhakade jadi visuddho || 486 || yathā matsyānāṃ prakṛte madanodake matsyā hi majjaṃti | na hi maṃḍūkā evaṃ paramārthakṛte yatiḥ viśuddhaḥ || 486 ||

piṇḍaśuddhi-adhikāra 6| . 191 sādhu āraṃbharūpa adhaḥkarmase utpanna hue bhī āhārako grahaṇa karatā hai taubhī vaha śuddha hai karmabaṃdha nahīṃ hotā || 487 || samvovi piṃḍadoso vve bhāve samāsado duviho | davyagado puṇa dave bhāvagado appapariṇāmo ||488|| sarvaḥ api piṃḍadoṣaḥ dravye bhāva samāsato dvividhaḥ| dravyagato punaḥ dravye bhāvagato ātmapariṇāmaḥ || 488||
artha-sabhī piṃḍadoṣake saṃkṣapase do bheda haiṃ dravyagata bhāvagata | dravyameṃ jo rahatā hai vaha dravyagata hai aura apane pariṇāmoṃmeṃ jo malinatā hai vaha bhāvagata hai || 488 ||
āge dravyakā bheda kahate haiṃsavvesaṇaṃ ca viddesaṇaṃ ca suddhāsaṇaṃ ca te kamaso| esaṇasamidivisuddhaṃ ṇiviyaḍamavaṃjaṇaṃ jāṇe ||489|| sarvaiṣaṇaṃ ca viddhaṣaṇaṃ ca śuddhāśanaṃ ca te kramaśaḥ | eṣaṇāsamitiviśuddhaṃ nirvikṛtamavyaṃjanaṃ jānīhi ||489||
artha- sarveṣaṇa vidvaiṣaṇa śuddhāsana kharūpa tīna prakāra dravya hai vaha kramase ina svarūpa hai ki jo eṣaṇāsamitise pavitra ho, vikṛtiyoṃse rahita ho aura vyaṃjana rahita ho vaha dravya prāsuka bhojana hotā hai || 489 || davvaṃ khettaṃ kālaṃ bhāvaṃ balavīriyaṃ ca ṇāūṇa |.. kujā eṣaṇasamirdi jahovadiṭuṃ jiṇamadammi ||490||
dravyaṃ kṣetraṃ kālaṃ bhāvaṃ balavīrya ca jñātvā | kuryāt eṣaṇāsamitiṃ yathopadiṣṭāṃ jinamate || 490 || artha-āhārādi dravya, anūpa ādi kṣetra, śīta ādi kāla,

āge phalake icchuka sūtrakāra prārthanā karate haiṃ; - jeṇeha piṃḍasuddhī uvadiṭṭhā jehiṃ dhāridā sammaṃ | te vīrasādhuvaggā tiradaṇasuddhiṃ mama disaṃtu || 501 || yairiha piṃḍaśuddhiḥ upadiṣṭā yaiḥ dhāritā samyak | te vīrasādhuvargāḥ triratnaśuddhiṃ mama diśaṃtu || 501 || artha - jinhoṃne yaha piṃḍaśuddhi upadeśī hai aura jinhoṃne yaha acchī taraha dhāraṇa kī hai ve śūravīra sādhūsamūha mujhe tīna ratnoṃkī śuddhi deṃ arthāt unake prasādase mere bhī darśana jñāna cāritrakī nirmalatā ho || 501 ||
isaprakāra ācāryaśrībaṭṭakeriviracita mūlācārakī hiṃdī bhāṣāṭīkā meṃ āhāraśuddhiko kahanevālā chaṭhā piṃḍaśuddhi-adhikāra samāpta huā || 6 ||

agnikara karma baṃdhake nāśa honepara tapāne yogya hoke śuddha dhāturūpa siddhapako prāpta hotā hai || 508 ||
sadā āyārabiddaṇḍa sadā āyariyaṃ care | āyāramāyāravaṃto āyario teṇa uccade || 509 || sadā ācāravit sadā ācaritaṃ caraḥ | ācāramācārayan ācāryaḥ tena ucyate || 509 ||
artha – jo sarvakāla saṃbaṃdhī ācārako jāne, hameśā ācaraṇa yogyako ācaraṇa karatā ho aura anya sādhuoṃko ācaraṇa karātā ho isaliye vaha ācārya kahā jātā hai || 509 || jamhā paṃcavihācāraṃ ācaraṃto pabhāsadi | āyariyāṇi deto āyario teṇa uccade || 510 || yasmāt paṃcavidhācāraṃ ācaran prabhāsate |
ācaritāni darśayan ācāryaḥ tena ucyate || 510 ||

201
āge āvaśyakakī niyukti ( śabdārtha) kahate haiṃ;ṇa vaso avaso avasassa kammamāvāsayaṃtti bodhavvā| juttitti uvāyatti yaṇiravayavā hodi ṇijuttī ||515 na vaśaḥ avaśaḥ avaśasya karma āvaśyakamiti boddhavyaṃ | yuktiriti upāya iti ca niravayavā bhavati niyuktiḥ||515||
artha-jo kaṣāya rāgadveṣa ādike vaśībhūta na ho vaha avaśa hai usa avaśakā jo ācaraṇa vaha āvaśyaka hai | tathā yukti upāyako kahate haiṃ jo akhaṃḍita yukti vaha niyukti hai āvaśyakakī jo niyukti ( saṃpūrṇa upāya ) vaha āvaśyaka niyukti hai || 515 ||

209 artha-jagatako prakāśa karanevāle uttamakṣamādi dharmatīrthake karanevāle sarvajña praśaṃsākarane yogya pratyakṣajñānī jineṃdradeva uttama ahaṃta mujhe bodhi (samyaktvasahita jñāna ) deṃ || isameṃ daśa guṇa kahe haiṃ unase stuti kī gaī hai || 539 ||

ṣaḍāvaśyakādhikāra 7| 211 kriyāvān nitya kāraṇa kartā sarvavyāpī dūsaremeṃ praveśa na honevāle koī dravya haiṃ aura koī inase ulaṭe arthāt apariṇāmī ādi haiṃ| āyāsaṃ sapadesaṃ uḍḍamaho tiriyalogaṃ ca | khettalogaṃ viyāṇāhi aṇaṃtajiṇadesitaṃ ||546|| ākāśaṃ sapradeśaṃ ūrdhvamadhaḥ tiryaglokaṃ ca | . kṣetralokaṃ vijānīhi anaṃtajinadeśitaṃ || 546 || artha-pradeśa sahita ākāśa ūrdhvaloka adholoka tiryaglokarūpa tīnaprakāra hai use kṣetraloka jānanā || 546 || jaṃ diḍhe saṃṭhāṇaṃ vvāṇa guṇāṇa pajayāṇaṃ ca | ciṇhalogaṃ viyāṇāhi aṇaṃtajiṇadesidaṃ || 547 ||

dravyodyotaḥ agniḥ caṃdraḥ sūryo maṇiścaiva || 552 || artha — prakāśake do bheda haiṃ dravya bhāva | agni caṃdramā sūrya ratna ye saba dravyaudyota haiṃ || 552 ||
bhāvajjovo ṇāṇaṃ jaha bhaṇiyaṃ savvabhāvada risīhiṃ | tassadupayogakaraṇe bhāvujjovoti ṇādavvo || 553 || bhāvodyoto jñānaṃ yathā bhaṇitaṃ sarvabhāvadarśibhiḥ | tasya tu upayogakaraṇe bhāvodyota iti jñātavyaḥ || 553 || artha - jñāna hai vahī bhāvaudyota hai aisā kevalī bhagavānane kahā hai | usa jñānake upayoga karanese khaparaprakāśapanā hai isīliye vaha jñāna bhāvaudyota hai aisā jānanā || 553 || paṃcaviho khalu bhaṇio bhāvujjovo ya jiṇavariṃdehiṃ | ābhiṇiohiyasudaohiṇāṇamaṇakevalaṃ ṇeyaṃ || 554|| paṃcavidhaḥ khalu bhaṇitaḥ bhāvodyotaśca jinavareṃdraiḥ | ābhinibodhikaśrutāvadhijñānamanaḥ kevalaṃ jñeyaṃ || 554 || artha - jinadevane bhāvodyotake pāṃca bheda kahe haiṃ-mati śruta avadhi manaḥparyaya kevalajñāna | aisā jānanā || 554 || dabvajjovojovo pasihaṇṇadi parimidami khettama | bhāvajjovojovo logālogaṃ payāsedi || 555 || dravyodyotaḥ udyotaḥ pratihanyate parimite kṣetre | bhāvodyota udyotaḥ lokālokaṃ prakāśayati || 555 || artha- dravyodyotarūpa udyota anya dravyase ruka jātā hai aura parimita ( maryādārūpa ) kṣetrameṃ rahatā hai tathā bhāvodyotarūpī udyota loka aloka sabako prakāśatā hai kisīse rukatā nahīṃ555

214
mūlācāralogassunovayarā davvujjoeṇa ṇa hu jiṇā hoti| bhāvujovayarā puṇa hoṃti jiṇavarā cauvvīsā||556|| lokasyodyotakarā dravyodyotena na khalu jinā bhavaṃti | bhāvodyotakarāḥ punaḥ bhavaṃti jinavarāḥ caturviṃśatiḥ||556

ṣaḍāvaśyakādhikāra 7| 215 artha-saṃtāpa śāṃta hotā hai tṛṣṇākā nāśa hotā hai malapaṃkakī śuddhi hotī hai ye tīna kārya hote haiṃ isaliye yaha dravya tīrtha hai || dasaṇaṇāṇacaritteṃ ṇijuttā jiṇavarā du savvepi | tihi kāraṇehiṃ juttā tahmā te bhāvado titthaṃ ||560|| darśanajñānacāritraiḥ niryuktā jinavarāstu sarvepi | tribhiḥ kāraṇaiḥ yuktāḥ tasmāt te bhāvatastīrtham ||560||
artha-sabhī jinadeva darśana jñāna cāritrakara saṃyukta haiṃ| ina tīna kāraṇoṃse yukta haiṃ isaliye ve jinadeva bhāvatīrtha haiṃ||560|| jidakohamāṇamāyā jidalohā teṇa te jiṇā hoti | haṃtā ariṃ ca jammaṃ arahaṃtā teṇa vucaṃti || 561||
jitakrodhamānamāyā jitalobhāḥ tena te jinā bhavaṃti | haṃtāraḥ arīṇāṃ ca janmanaḥ ahaṃtastena ucyate || 561 ||
artha-krodha māna māyā lobha ina kaṣāyoṃko jīta liyā hai isaliye ve bhagavān jina haiṃ | aura karmaśatruoṃke tathā saṃsārake nāśa karanevāle haiṃ isaliye arhata kahe jāte haiṃ || 561 || arihaṃti vaṃdaṇaṇamaṃsaṇāṇi arihaṃti pūyasakāraṃ | arihaṃti siddhigamaṇaṃ arahaṃtā teṇa uccati || 562||
arhati vaṃdanānamaskārayoḥ arhati pūjāsatkāraṃ | arhati siddhigamanaṃ arhataḥ tena ucyate || 562 ||
artha-vaṃdanā aura namaskārake yogya haiṃ pūjā aura satkārake yogya haiṃ mokṣa jāneke yogya haiṃ isa kāraṇa ve arhata kahe jāte haiṃ| kiha te ṇa kittaṇijjā sadevamaṇuyāsurehiṃ logehiṃ | dasaṇaṇāṇacaritte tava viṇao jehiṃ paṇṇatto||563||

ṣaḍāvaśyakādhikāra | 217 artha—aise pūrvokta viśeṣaṇoṃ sahita jineṃdradeva mujhe janmamaraṇarūpa rogase rahita kareṃ tathā bheda jñānakī prāpti aura samādhimaraṇa deṃ| kyā yaha nidāna hai yahāṃ vikalpase samajhanā || 566 ||

kṛtikarma citakarma pūjākarma ca vinayakarma ca |
kartavyaṃ kena kasya vā kathaṃ vā kasmin vā kṛtikṛtvaḥ ||576 kiyaṃtyavanatāni kati śirāṃsi katibhiḥ āvartakaiḥ pariśuddhaṃ | katidoṣavipramuktaṃ kṛtikarma bhavati kartavyaṃ || 577 ||
220
artha — jisase āṭha prakārake karmoṃkā chedana ho vaha kṛtikarma hai, jisase puṇyakarmakā saṃcaya ho vaha citakarma hai, jisase pūjā karanā vaha mālā caṃdana ādi pūjākarma hai, śuśrūṣākā karanā vinayakarma hai | vaha kriyā karma kauna kare kisakā karanā kisa vidhise karanā kisa avasthāmeṃ karanā kitanī vāra karanā | kitanī avanatiyoṃse karanā kitanī vāra mastakameṃ hātha rakhakara karanā kitane āvartīse śuddha hotā hai kitane doṣoṃ rahita kṛtikarma karanā | isaprakāra praśnoṃpara vicāra karanā cāhiye ||576/577|| kṛtikarma vinayakā ekārtha hai isaliye vinayakī nirukti karate haiṃ;jāviṇedi kammaṃ aṭṭhavihaṃ cāuraṃgamokho ya | tā vadaṃti viduso viṇaoti vilīṇasaṃsārā 578 yasmāt vinayati karma aṣṭavidhaṃ cāturaṃgamokṣaśca | tasmāt vadaṃti vidvāṃso vinaya iti vilīnasaṃsārāḥ || 578 artha — jisakāraṇa āṭha prakārake karmoṃkā nāśa karatā hai caturgatirūpa saṃsārase mokṣa karatā hai isakāraṇase saṃsāra se pāra hue paṃḍita puruṣa usako vinaya kahate haiṃ || 578 || puvvaṃ caiva ya viṇao paruvido jiṇavarehiṃ savvehiṃ | sāsu kammabhūmisu ṇiccaṃ so mokkhamaggammi || 579 ||

ṣaḍāvaśyakādhikāra 7|| pūrvamin caiva vinayaḥ prarūpito jinavaraiḥ sarvaiḥ | sarvāsu karmabhūmiṣu nityaṃ sa mokṣamārge || 579 ||

mūlācāraprāyake anukūla bolanā, deśa yogya kālayogya apanā dravya denāye saba lokānuvṛtti vinaya hai | apane prayojanakeliye hātha joḍa़nā arthanimitta vinaya hai || 582 || emeva kāmataṃte bhayaviṇao ceva āṇupuvvīe | paṃcamao khalu viṇao parūvaṇā tassimā hodi||583

ṣaḍāvaśyakādhikāra 7| 223 ṇāṇī gacchadi ṇāṇī vaṃcadiṇāṇīṇavaṃ ca ṇādiyādi| ṇāṇeṇa kuṇadi caraṇaṃ tahmā ṇāṇe have viṇao||586||
jñānī gacchati jñānī vaṃcati jñānī navaṃ ca nādadāti | jñānena karoti caraṇaṃ tasmāt jñāne bhavet vinayaḥ ||586||
artha-jñānī mokṣako jānatā hai jñānī pāpako choḍa़tā hai jñānī navīna karmoṃko grahaṇa nahīṃ karatā, jñānī cāritrako aṃgīkāra karatā hai isaliye jñānameṃ vinaya arthāt jñānavinaya karanā cāhiye| porāṇaya kammarayaṃ cariyā rittaṃ karedi jadamāṇo | ṇavakammaṃ ṇa ya baṃdhadi carittaviṇaotti ṇāvvo587 paurāṇaṃ karmarajaḥ caryayā riktaṃ karoti yatamānaḥ | navakarma na ca banāti caritravinaya iti jñātavyaḥ ||587||
artha-yatnācāra sahita pravartatā jñānī cāritrase purāne karmoṃrūpa dhūlīkā kṣaya karatā hai aura navīnakarmoṃko bāṃdhatā nahīṃ hai yahī cāritra-vinaya hai aisā jānanā || 587 || avaṇayadi taveṇa tamaṃ uvaṇayadi mokkhamaggamappāṇaṃ | tavaviṇayaṇiyamidamadīso tavaviṇaotti ṇādavyo |
apanayati tapasā tamaḥ upanayati mokṣamārgamātmānaṃ | tapovinayaniyamitamatiḥ sa tapovinaya iti jñātavyaḥ 588
artha-jisakī tapavinayameṃ buddhi dṛḍha hai aisā puruṣa tapase pāparūpī aṃdhakārako haṭātā hai ātmāko mokṣamārgameṃ prāpta karatā hai yahī tapavinaya hai aisā jānanā || 588 || tamā savvapayatte viṇayattaṃ mā kadāi chaṃḍijjo| appasudo viya puriso khavedi kammāṇi viṇaeṇa589

āge pratikramaṇaniryuktikā kharūpa kahate haiṃ; ṇāmavaṇā davve khette kāle tadheva bhāve ya | eso paḍikamaṇage ṇikkhevo chavviho ṇeo || 612 || nāmasthāpanā dravyaṃ kṣetraṃ kālastathaiva bhāvaśca | eṣa pratikramaṇake nikṣepaḥ ṣaḍvidho jñeyaḥ || 612 || artha – nāma sthāpanā dravya kṣetra kāla aura bhāva-ye chaha pratikramaṇake nikṣepa jānanā || jaise doṣoṃke nāmakī nivṛtti karanā nāmapratikramaṇa hai | isītaraha anya bhī samajha lenā || 612 || paḍikamaṇaṃ devasiyaṃ rādiya iriyāpadhaṃ ca bodhavvaṃ | pakkhiya cādummā siya saṃvaccharamuttamahaṃ ca || 613 ||

233
kṛtvā ca kṛtikarma pratilekhya aṃjalīkaraṇaśuddhaḥ| ālocayet suvihitaḥ gauravaṃ mānaṃ ca muktvā || 618 ||
artha-vinayakarma karake, śarīra āsanako pīchī va netrase śuddha karake, aṃjalikriyāmeṃ śuddha huā nirmala pravṛttivālā sādhu ṛddhi ādi gaurava aura jāti ādike mānako choḍa़kara guruse apane aparādhoṃkā nivedana kare || 618 || ālocaṇaṃ divasiyaṃ rādia iriyāvadhaṃ ca bodhavvaṃ | pakkhiya cādummāsiya saṃvaccharamuttamaṭuṃ ca || 619 ||
ālocanaṃ daivasikaṃ rātrika īryāpathaṃ ca boddhavyaṃ | pākṣikaṃ cāturmāsikaṃ sāṃvatsarikamuttamārtha ca || 619 ||
artha-guruke samīpa aparādhakā kahanā vaha ālocanā hai| vaha daivasika rātrika īryāpathika pākṣika caturmāsika saṃvatsarika uttamārtha-isataraha sātaprakārakā jānanā cāhiye || 619|| aṇābhogakidaṃ kammaṃ jaṃ kiṃvi maṇasā kadaṃ | taṃ savvaṃ ālocejahu avvākhitteṇa cedasā ||620||
anābhogakṛtaṃ karma yat kimapi manasā kṛtaṃ | tat sarva ālocayet avyākṣiptena cetasā || 620 || artha-anyako nahīṃ mālūma aisā anābhogarūpa kiyā gayā atīcāra, jo kucha manase kiyā gayā karma usa sabako nirākulacittase guruke sāmane ālocana ( nivedana ) kare || 620 || ālocaṇamāluṃcaṇa vigaḍīkaraṇaṃ ca bhāvasuddhī du| ālocidahmi ārādhaṇā aṇālocaṇe bhajjā ||621|| ālocanamālucanaṃ vikṛtikaraṇaṃ ca bhāvaśuddhistu |

artha-ālocana ālaṃcana vikṛtikaraṇa aura bhāvaśuddhi ye ekārtha haiṃ| guruke sāmane nivedana karanese samyagdarśanādikī śuddhi hotī hai aura doṣoṃke nahīṃ kahanepara śuddhi hotī bhī hai athavā nahīṃ bhī hotī || 621 || uppaṇṇo uppaṇṇā māyā aṇupuvvaso ṇihaṃtavvā | ālocaṇaṇiṃdaṇagarahaṇāhiṃṇa puṇo tiaṃbidi||622 utpanna utpannā māyā anupūrvaśo nihaṃtavyā | ālocananiṃdanagarhaṇe na punaḥ tṛtīyaṃ dvitīyaṃ || 622 || artha-jaise jaise kramase atīcāra lage usī kramase kuṭilatā choḍa़ atīcāra śuddha karanā cāhiye | aura una doṣoṃko guruke sāmane kahe anyake sāmane prakaṭa kare athavā vayaṃ niṃdā kare paraṃtu usīdina kare dūsare tīsare dina na kare || 622 || ālocaṇāṇaṃdaṇagarahaṇāhiṃ abbhuṭṭio a karaṇāya | taṃ bhāvapaḍikkamaṇaṃ sesaṃ puṇa vvado bhaṇiaṃ||623||
ālocananiṃdanagahaṇaiḥ abhyutthitaśca karaṇe | tat bhāvapratikramaṇaṃ śeṣaṃ punaḥ dravyato bhaṇitaṃ || 623 ||
artha-ālocana niṃdana garhaṇa ina tīnoṃkara pratikramaṇakriyāmeṃ udyamī huā sādhu vaha bhāvapratikramaṇa hai aura isase anya dravyapratikramaṇa hai || 623 || bhāveṇa aṇuvajutto davvībhūdo paḍikkamadi jo du | jassarṭa paḍikamade taṃ puṇa aha~ ṇa sādhedi || 624 ||
bhāvena anupayuktaḥ dravyībhūtaḥ pratikramate yastu |

ṣaḍāvaśyakādhikāra 7| 235 yasyārtha pratikramate taṃ punaḥ artha na sādhayati || 624 ||
artha-śuddha pariṇāmoṃse rahita huā doṣoṃse ghṛṇā nahīṃ karatā sādhu jisa doṣake dūra karaneke liye pratikramaṇa karatā hai usa prayojanako phira vaha nahīṃ sādhasakatā || 624 || bhāveṇa saṃpajutto jadatthajogo ya jaṃpade suttaṃ | so kammaṇijarāe viulāe vade sādhū || 625 || bhāvena saṃprayuktaḥ yadarthayogaśca jalpati sūtraṃ | sa karmanirjarāyāṃ vipulāyāṃ vartate sādhuḥ || 625 ||
artha-bhāvakara saṃyukta sādhu jisa nimitta śubha ācaraṇa karatā huā pratikramaṇapadako uccāraṇa karatā hai vaha sādhu bahuta karmoṃkī nirjarā karanemeṃ pravartatā hai || 625 || sapaḍikkamaṇo dhammo purimassa ya pacchimassa ya jiṇassa aparādhe paḍikamaṇaṃ majjhimayāṇaṃ jiṇavarāṇaṃ ||26|| sapratikramaṇo dharmaḥ pūrvasya ca paścimasya ca jinasya | aparādhe pratikramaṇaṃ madhyamānāṃ jinavarāṇāṃ || 626||
artha-pahale ṛṣabhadeva tīrthakarake samayameṃ tathā pichale mahāvīra tīrthakarake samayameṃ pratikramaṇa sahita dharma pravartatā hai aura bīcake ajitanātha ādi tīrthaṃkaroṃke samayameṃ aparādha ho to pratikramaṇa hotā hai kyoṃki bahuta aparādha nahīṃ hotā || 626 || jāvedu appaṇo vā aṇṇadare vā bhave adīcāro| tāvedu paḍikkamaṇaṃ majjhimayāṇaṃ jiṇavarāṇaṃ || 627 ||
yasin ātmano vā anyatarasya vā bhavedatīcāraḥ | tasin pratikramaṇaṃ madhyamānāṃ jinavarāṇāṃ || 627 ||

artha - jisa vrata meṃ apane athavā anyake atīcāra lagatā ho usa vratake atīcāra meṃ bīcake tīrthakaroṃ ke samayameṃ pratikramaṇa hai || iriyāgoyarasumiṇā disavvamācaradu mā va ācaradu | purima carimādu savve savvaṃ ṇiyamā paḍikamaṃdi || 628 || īryāgocarakhamādisarva ācaratu mā vā ācaratu | pūrve carame tu sarve sarvān niyamān pratikramate || 628 || artha - ṛṣabhadeva va mahāvīra prabhuke śiṣya ina saba īyagocarī svapnādise utpanna hue atīcāroṃko prāpta ho athavā mata prāpta ho taubhī pratikramaṇake saba daṃḍakoṃ ko uccāraṇa karate haiṃ || 628 || majjhimayā dibuddhī eyaggamaṇā amoha lakkhā ya | tahmā hu jamācaraṃti taṃ garahaṃtā vi sujjhaṃti || 629 ||

243
aṃtara hai aise samapāda, saba hātha ādi aṃgoṃkā calanā jisameṃ nahīṃ hai vaha śuddha kāyotsarga hai || 650 || mukkhaṭṭhī jidaṇiddo suttatthavisārado karaṇasuddho| ādabalaviriyajutto kāussaggī visuddhappā ||651|| mokṣārthī jitanidraḥ sūtrārthaviśāradaḥ karaṇaśuddhaḥ| ātmabalavīryayuktaḥ kāyotsargī viśuddhātmā || 651 ||

244
mūlācāraṣaḍjīvanikāyaiḥ bhayamadasthānaḥ brahmadharme | kāyotsarga adhitiṣṭhāmi tatkarmanighātanārtha || 654 ||
artha-eka pādase jo khaḍa़ā hai usake rāgadveṣakara jo atīcāra ho usītaraha cāra kaṣāyoṃkara tīna guptiyoṃkā jo ulaṃghana ho, vratoṃmeṃ jo atīcāra ho, pṛthivī ādi chaha kāya jīvoṃkī virādhanāse jo atīcāra huā ho, sāta bhaya āṭha bhedoṃke dvārā jo atīcāra huā ho, brahmacarya dharmameṃ jo atīcāra huā ho-ina sabase āyā jo karma usake nāśake liye maiṃ kāyotsargakā āśraya letā hūṃ arthāt kāyotsargase tiṣṭhatā hūṃ || 653-654 || je keī uvasaggā devāmāṇusatirikkhacedaṇiyā| te savve adhiāse kāosagge ṭhido saṃto||655||
ye kecana upasargā devamānuṣatiryagacetanikāḥ| tān sarvān adhyāse kāyotsarge sthitaḥ san || 655 ||
artha-jo kucha deva manuṣya tiryaṃca acetanakṛta upasarga haiṃ una sabako kāyotsargameṃ sthita huā maiṃ acchītaraha sahana karatā hūṃ || 655 || saṃvaccharamukkassaṃ bhiṇṇamuhuttaṃ jahaṇṇayaṃ hodi | sesā kāosaggā hoti aṇegesu ṭhāṇesu || 656 || saṃvatsaramutkṛṣṭaṃ bhinnamuhūrta jaghanyaṃ bhavati | śeṣāḥ kāyotsargā bhavaṃti anekeṣu sthāneṣu || 656 ||
artha-kāyotsarga ekavarṣakā utkṛṣṭa aura aṃtarmuhūrta pramāṇa jaghanya hotā hai| śeṣa kāyotsarga dinarātri ādike bhedase bahuta haiṃ| asadaṃ devasiyaṃ kalladdhaṃ pakkhiyaṃ ca tiṇṇisayā|

mūlācāra
prakāra jinaśāsanameṃ mānāgayā saba saṃkalpa hai usako śubhadhyāna tuma jāno || 678-680 taka ||

ṣaḍāvaśyakādhikāra 7| 253 āge ṣaḍāvaśyaka cūlikāko kahate haiṃ;savāvāsaṇijutto ṇiyamā siddhotti hoi ṇāyavvo| aha ṇissesaṃ kuṇadi ṇa ṇiyamā āvāsayā hoṃti684 sarvāvaśyakaniyuktaḥ niyamāt siddha iti bhavati jñātavyaḥ | atha niśśeṣāṇi karoti na niyamāt āvāsakā bhavaṃti684

mūlācāra
artha-isa saṃsārameṃ kaṣṭase milanevāle apaneko iṣṭa padārtha milane kaṭhina haiṃ, māraṇa baṃdhana ādi bhayasahita haiṃ, thoḍa़e kāla rahanevāle haiṃ sārarahita haiṃ | aura sevana kiyegaye kāmabhoga bhī duḥkhake hī denevāle haiṃ isaliye aśubha haiṃ || 722 || asuiciavile ganbhe vasamāṇo vatthipaḍalapacchaṇṇo| mādūisebhalālāiyaṃ tu tivvāsuhaṃ pibadi || 723 ||
aśucyāvile garbhe vasan vastipaṭalapracchannaḥ| mātṛśleṣmalālāpitaṃ tu tīvrāśubhaṃ pibati || 723||
artha-yaha jīva mūtramalayukta garbhameṃ vasatā jarāyu (jera) kara lipaṭā huā mātāke bhakṣaṇase utpanna śleṣmā lārakara sahita tīvra durgadha rasako pītā hai || 723 || maṃsahisebhavasaruhiracammapittaṃtamuttakuṇipakuḍi | bahudukkharogabhāyaṇa sarīramasubhaṃ viyāṇāhi || 724 māṃsāsthiśleṣmavasārudhiracarmapittāṃtramūtrakuṇipakuṭīṃ | bahuduḥkharogabhājanaṃ śarīramaśubhaṃ vijānīhi || 724 ||
artha-māṃsa hāḍa kapha meda lohī cāma pitta āṃta mūtra mala inakā ghara, bahuta duḥkha aura rogoṃkā pātra aise śarīrako tuma aśuci jāno || 724 || atthaṃ kāmasarīrādigaṃpi savvamasubhatti ṇāūṇa | ṇivijaṃto jhāyasu jaha jahasi kalevaraṃ asuī|725
artha kāmaśarīrādikamapi sarvamaśubhamiti jñātvā | nirvedyamānaḥ dhyāya yathā jahāsi kalevaraṃ aśuci ||725|| 1. artha-strī vastra dhanādi maithuna śarīrādi ye sabhī aśubha haiṃ

dvādaśānuprekṣādhikāra 8|
275 dhanyāstaṃ pratipannā viśuddhamanasā jagati manujāḥ ||750||

labdheṣvapi eteṣu ca bodhiḥ jinaśāsane na hi sulabhā | kupathānāmākulatvāt yat baliṣṭhau rāgadveṣau ca || 757 ||

anagārabhāvanādhikāra 9| 281 kāṃcanapriyaṃguvidrumaghanakuṃdamṛṇālavarṇān ||767 || anagāramaharṣīṇāṃ nāgeṃdranareṃdredramahitānāṃ | vakṣyāmi vividhasāraṃ bhāvanāsūtraṃ guṇamahat || 768 ||

anagārabhāvanādhikāra 9 |
283
artha — asthira nāśasahita isa jīvanako aura paramārtharahita isa manuṣyajanmako jānakara strī ādi upabhoga tathā bhojana ādi bhogoṃse abhilāṣārahita hue, nirgrathādisvarūpa cāritra meṃ dṛḍha buddhivāle, gharake rahanese virakta cittavāle aise vīrapuruṣa bhogameṃ āye phūloṃkī taraha gāya ghoḍa़ā ādi dhana sonā inakara paripūrṇa aise bāṃdhava janoṃ ko choḍa़ dete haiṃ || 773|774 || jammaṇamaraṇuvviggā bhīdā saṃsāravāsamasubhassa | rocaṃti jiṇavaramadaṃ pavayaṇaṃ vaḍamāṇassa || 775 || janmamaraṇodvinā bhītāḥ saṃsāravāse aśubhāt | rocaṃte jinavaramataṃ pravacanaṃ vardhamānasya || 775 ||
artha — janma aura maraṇase kaṃpita tathā saṃsāra vāsameṃ duḥkhase bhayabhīta muni vṛṣabhādi jinavara ke matakī vardhamāna svāmīke dvādaśāṃga caturdaśa pūrvasvarūpa pravacanakī śraddhā karate haiṃ | 775 || pavaravaradhammatitthaṃ jiṇavaravasahassa vaḍamāṇassa | tiviheṇa saddahaṃti ya ṇatthi ido uttaraṃ aṇṇaṃ || 776 || pravaravaradharmatīrthaṃ jinavaravṛṣabhasya varṣamānasya | trividhena śraddadhati ca nāsti ita uttaramanyat || 776 || artha — vṛṣabhadeva va mahāvīra khāṃmī ina saba tīrthakaroṃke ati śreṣṭha dharmarūpī tīrthako manavacanakāyakī śuddhatāse śraddhāna karate haiṃ| kyoṃki isatīrthase adhika anyatīrtha koī nahīṃ hai || 776 || ucchāhaṇicchidamadī vavasidavavasāyabaddhakacchā ya | bhāvāṇurāyaratā jiṇapaṇṇattammi dhammammi || 777 || utsāha niścitamatayo vyavasitavyavasāyabaddhakakṣāśra |

artha-satyavacana ahiṃsā acaurya brahmacaryakā pālana aura parigrahatyāga ina pāṃca mahāvratoṃko acchī taraha cāhate haiṃ ||779|| pāṇivaha musāvā, adatta mehuṇa pariggahaṃ ceva | tiviheṇa paḍikaṃte jāvajīvaṃ diḍhadhidīyā ||780||

285
artha - sthira buddhivāle sādhu hiṃsā, jhūṭhabolanā corī maithunasevā parigraha ina pāṃca pāpoṃko manavacanakāyase jīvanaparyaṃta tyāgate haiṃ || 780 ||

te savvasaṃgamukkā amamā apariggahā jahājādā | vosacantadehā jiṇavaradhammaṃ samaṃ ṇeṃti || 781 || te sarvasaṃgamuktā amamā aparigrahā yathājātāḥ | vyutsṛṣṭatyaktadehā jinavaradharma samaṃ nayaṃti || 781 || artha — ve muni saba aṃtaraṃga parigraharahita hue, sneharahita, kṣetrādi bāhya parigraharahita, nagnamudrāko prāpta taila snānādi dehasaṃskārase rahita hue jinadharma jo cāritra usako paralokameṃ bhī sātha lejāte haiṃ || 781 ||
savāraṃbhaṇiyattā juttā jiṇadesidammi dhammammi | ṇa ya icchaṃti mamattiṃ pariggahe bālamittammi || 782 || sarvāraṃbhanivṛttā yuktā jinadeśite dharme |
na ca icchaṃti mamatvaṃ parigrahe bālamātre || 782 ||

anagārabhāvanādhikāra 9| eyāiṇo avihalā vasaṃti girikaṃdaresu sappurisā| dhīrā adīṇamaṇasā ramamāṇā vīravayaṇammi ||787||
ekākinaḥ avihvalā vasaṃti girikaṃdareṣu satpuruṣāḥ| dhīrā adīnamanaso ramamāṇā vīravacane || 787 ||
artha–sahāyatārahita utsāhasahita dhīra vīra dīnavṛttirahita mahāvīrakhāmīke vacanoṃmeṃ ramate hue aise śreṣṭha muni pahāḍa़kī guphā. oṃmeṃ rahate haiṃ || 787 || vasadhisu appaḍibaddhā ṇa te mamattiṃ kareṃti vasadhīsu| suṇṇāgāramasāṇe vasaṃti te vīravasadīsu|| 788 || vasatiṣu apratibaddhā na te mamatvaṃ kurvati vasatiṣu | śūnyāgāramaśāneṣu vasaṃti te vīravasatiṣu || 788 ||
artha-vasatikāoṃ mamatārahita abhiprāyavāle ve sādhu vasa. tikāoṃmeṃ mamatā nahīṃ karate aura vīrapuruṣoṃke rahaneke sthāna aise śūnyasthāna smaśānabhūmi ādi sthāna unameṃ rahate haiṃ || 788 || panbhārakaṃdaresu a kāpurisabhayaṃkaresu sappurisā| vasadhī abhirocaṃti ya sāvadabahughoragaṃbhīrā ||789||
prāgbhārakaṃdareṣu ca kāpuruṣabhayaṃkareṣu satpuruṣāḥ | vasatimabhirocaṃte śvāpadabahughoragaṃbhīrāḥ || 789 ||

297 payaṇaṃ va pāyaṇaṃ vā ṇa kareṃti aṇeva te karāveṃti | payaṇāraṃbhaṇiyattā saṃtuṭṭhā bhikkhametteṇa || 819|| pacanaṃ vā pācanaṃ vā na kurveti ca naiva te kārayati | pacanāraṃbhanivṛttāḥ saṃtuṣṭā bhikṣāmātreṇa || 819 ||

mūlācāra
bhāṣāṃ vinayavihīnāṃ dharmavirodhi vivarjayaṃti vacanaṃ | pṛṣṭamapṛṣṭaṃ vā nāpi te bhāṣate satpuruṣāḥ|| 853||
artha-satpuruṣa ve muni vinayarahita kaṭhora bhāṣāko tathā dharmase viruddha vacanoṃko choḍa़ dete haiṃ| aura anya bhī virodha karanevāle vacanoṃko kabhī nahīṃ bolate || 853 || acchīhiṃa pecchaṃtā kaṇṇehiṃya bahuvihāya suṇamāṇā| atthaṃti mūyabhūyā ṇa te karaṃti huloiyakahāo||854
akṣibhiḥ paśyaṃtaḥ karṇaiḥ ca bahuvidhāni śṛṇvaṃtaḥ| tiṣṭhaṃti mūkabhūtā na te kurvati hi laukikakathāḥ ||854||
artha-ve sādhu netroṃse saba yogya ayogyako dekhate haiṃ aura kānoṃse saba tarahake śabdoṃko sunate haiṃ paraṃtu ve gūṃgeke samāna tiṣṭhate haiṃ laukikīkathā nahīṃ karate || 854 || itthikahā atthakahā bhattakahā kheḍakavaḍāṇaṃ ca | rāyakahā corakahā jaṇavadaṇayarāyarakahāo ||855|| svīkathā arthakathā bhaktakathā kheṭakarvaṭayośca | rājakathā corakathā janapadanagarākarakathāḥ || 855||
artha-strī saṃbaṃdhī kathā dhanakathā bhojanakathā nadīparvatase ghirāhuā sthāna usakī kathā parvatase hī ghirā huā sthāna usakī kathā rājakathā corakathā deśa nagara kathā khāni saṃbaṃdhī kathā 855 ṇaḍabhaḍamallakahāo māyākarajallamuṭṭhiyāṇaṃ ca | ajaulalaṃghiyāṇaṃ kahāsu ṇa virajae dhīrā || 856|| naṭabhaṭamallakathāḥ māyākarajallamuṣṭikayośca | āyokulalaṃpikānāṃ kathāsu nāpi rajyaṃte dhīrāḥ||856||

anagārabhāvanādhikāra 9 |
309
artha —naṭakathā bhaṭakathā mallakathā, kapaṭake bheṣase jīnevāle vyāgha aura jvārī inakī kathā, hiṃsāmeṃ rata rahanevāloṃ kī kathā, vāṃsapara caḍhanevāle naṭoṃ kī kathā - ye saba laukikī kathā ( vikathā ) haiṃ inameṃ vairāgī munirāja rāgabhāva nahīṃ karate || 856 || vikahāvisottiyāṇaṃ khaṇamavi hidaeṇa te ṇa ciṃtaṃti | dhamme laddhamadīyā vikahā tiviheṇa vajjaṃti || 857 || vikathāviśrutīn kṣaṇamapi hṛdayena te na ciṃtayaṃti | dharme labdhamatayaḥ vikathāḥ trividhena varjayaṃti || 857 || artha - strīkathā ādi vikathā aura mithyāśāstra inako ve muni manase bhī citavana nahīṃ karate | dharmameṃ prāpta buddhivāle muni vikathāko manavacanakāyase choḍa़ dete haiṃ || 857 || kukkuya kaṃdappāiya hāsa ullāvaṇaṃ ca kheḍaṃ ca | madaduṣpahatthavahiṃ ṇa kareṃti muṇī ṇa kāreṃti || 858 || kautkucyaṃ kaṃdarpāyitaṃ hāsyaṃ ullāpanaṃ ca kheḍaṃ ca | madadarpahastatāḍa़naṃ na kurvati munayaḥ na kārayati || 858 || artha – hṛdaya kaṃṭhase apragaṭa śabdakā karanā, kāmake upajānevāle hāsyamile vacana, hāsyavacana, anekacaturāī sahita mīṭhe vacana, parako ṭhaganerūpa vacana, madake garvase hāthakā tāḍa़nāinako munirāja na to karate haiṃ aura na dūsarese karāte haiṃ || 858 || te hoṃti ṇivviyārā thimidamadī padiṭṭidā jahā udadhī | ṇiyamesu daḍhavvadiṇo pārattavimaggayā samaṇā || 859||
te bhavaṃti nirvikārāḥ stimitamatayaḥ pratiṣṭhitāḥ yathā udadhiḥ | niyameṣu dṛḍhavratinaḥ pāratryavimārgakāḥ śramaṇāḥ || 859 ||

anagārabhāvanādhikāra 9| 311 pramāda rahita hote haiṃ tapa cāritra aura teraha prakāra karaṇoṃmeṃ udyamī hue pāpoṃke nāśa karanevāle hote haiṃ || 862 || hemaṃta dhidimaṃtā sahaṃti te himarayaṃ paramaghoraṃ | aṃgesu ṇivaḍamāṇaṃ ṇaliṇivaṇaviṇāsayaṃ sīyaṃ||863|| hemaṃte dhṛtimaṃtaḥ sahate te himarajaḥ paramaghoraṃ | aṃgeṣu nipatat nalinīvanavināśakaṃ śītaṃ || 863 ||

anagārabhāvanādhikāra 9| yamarāja ( kāla ) manuṣyoṃse gussā karasakatā hai arthāt mṛtyu bhī usako nahīṃ jīta sakatī || 868 || jadivi ya kareṃti pāvaṃ ede jiṇavayaṇabāhirā purisā| taṃ savvaṃ sahidavvaṃ kammāṇa khayaṃ karateṇa || 869 ||

anagārabhāvanādhikāra 9| 315 nakara chūṭā huā nagarakī saḍa़ka para atisāmarthyavāle manuṣyakara tīkṣṇa ( maiṃne ) aṃkuśase vaśa kiyā jātā hai || 874 || taha caṃḍo maṇahatthī uddāmo viṣayarāyamaggammi | ṇāṇaṃkuseṇa dhario ruddho jaha mattahatthivva || 875 ||

mūlācāra
artha-jisakā saṃtoṣameṃ atyaṃta niścitamati honerūpa arthāt tattvarucirūpa to parakoṭā hai, cāritra baḍa़ā daravājā hai, upaśamabhāva aura dharma ye do jisake kivāḍa़ haiṃ aura doprakārakā saṃyama vaha rakṣākaranevālā kotavāla hai aisā taparūpī nagara hai || 877 || rāgo doso moho iṃdiya corā ya ujjadā ṇicaṃ | ṇa ca eti pahaṃsetuṃ sappurisasurakkhiyaṃ ṇayaraṃ ||878||

artha-ye iṃdriyarūpī ghoḍe khābhāvika rāgadveṣakara prere hue dharmadhyānarūpī rathako viṣayarūpī kumārgameṃ lejāte haiṃ isaliye ekāgramanarūpī lagāmako balavān ( majabūta ) karo || 879 || rāgo doso moho dhidīe dhīrehiṃ ṇijidā samma | paṃciṃdiyā ya daṃtā vadovavāsappahārehiṃ || 880||

anagārabhāvanādhikāra 9| 317 artha-saṃjamī muniyoṃne rāga dveṣa moha ye to ratnatrayameṃ dṛḍha bhāvanārūpa dhṛtise acchītaraha jīta liye aura vrata upavāsarūpī hathiyāroṃkara pāṃca iṃdriyoṃko vaśameṃ kiyā || 880 || daṃteṃdiyā maharisī rāgaṃ dosaṃ ca te khavedūṇaṃ | jhāṇovaogajuttā khaveṃti kammaṃ khavidamohā ||881||

artha - kapaḍa़e ādi saba parigrahakā tyāga, keśaloṃca, śarīrasaṃskārakā abhāva morapīchī yaha cāraprakāra liṃgabheda jānanā | ye cāroṃ aparigraha samīcīna bhāvanā vītarāgatā dayāpālanā inake cinha haiṃ || 908 ||

artha-uttamabhaktivāle puruṣakara diyā gayā, śarīrake yogya, prāsuka navakoṭikara śuddha niravadya kutsādidoṣarahita eṣaṇāsamitikara śuddha daśa doṣoṃkara rahita caudaha maloṃkara varjita aise āhārako dravyapramāṇa kṣetra kāla bhāvoṃko jāna kara khāya || 943-944 || āhāredu tavassī vigadiṃgālaṃ vigadadhūmaṃ ca| jattāsāhaṇamattaṃ javaṇāhāraṃ vigadarāgo||945||
āharet tapasvī vigatāṃgāraṃ vigatadhūmaṃ ca | yātrāsādhanamātraṃ yavanāhāraṃ vigatarāgaḥ ||945 ||
artha-aṃgāra doṣarahita dhūmadoṣarahita samyagdarśanādi rakṣāke nimitta kṣudhāke upaśama karanemātra āhārako vītarāgī muni grahaṇa kare || 945 || vavahārasohaṇāe paramaṭṭhāe tahā pariharau| ..

samayasārādhikāra 10| duvihā cāvi dugaṃchā loiya loguttarā ceva || 946 ||

aura vahāṃpara niṣadyā ( āsana ) śayana khādhyāya āhāra aura pratikramaṇa karanā yogya nahīṃ hai || 952 || hodi duguṃchā duvihā vavahārādo tadhā ya paramaho| payadeṇa ya paramahe vavahāreṇa ya tahā pacchā || 953 ||

342
mūlācāraālasī pīche doṣa kahanevālā piśuna ho, gurutā kaṣāya bahuta rakhatā ho aisā sādhu sevane yogya nahīṃ hai || 955 || vejāvaccavihīṇaṃ viṇayavihūṇaṃ ca dussudikusīlaṃ | samaṇaṃ virāgahīṇaṃ susaṃjamo sādhu ṇa sevija||956||

samayasārādhikāra 10|
347 evaṃ sasūtrapuruṣo na naśyati tathā pramādadoṣeṇa || 971 ||
artha-jaise suī sūkṣma bhī pramādadoṣase kūḍemeṃ girī huī ḍorākara sahita huī naṣṭa nahīṃ hotī hai dekhanese milajātī hai usītaraha śāstrakhādhyāyayukta puruṣa bhī pramādadoṣase utkṛṣṭa tapa rahita huā bhī saṃsārarūpī gaḍḍhe meṃ nahīṃ paḍa़tā || 971 || ṇiiṃ jiṇehi ṇicaṃ ṇiddā khalu ṇaramacedaṇaṃ kuṇadi| vaheja hū pasūto samaṇo savvesu dosesu ||972 || nidrāṃ jaya nityaṃ nidrā khalu naramacetanaṃ karoti | varteta hi prasuptaḥ śramaṇaḥ sarveṣu doṣeṣu || 972 ||
artha-he sādhu tū nidrāko jīta kyoṃki nidrā manuṣyako vivekarahita acetana banā detī hai| sotā huā muni saba doṣoṃmeṃ pravartatā hai || 972 || jaha usugāro usumujju kuṇaī saṃpiḍiyehiṃ ṇayaṇehiṃ | taha sāhū bhāvejo cittaṃ eyaggabhāveṇa || 973 ||
yathā iṣukāra iṣu ṛju karoti saṃpiḍitābhyāṃ nayanābhyāṃ | tathā sādhuḥ bhāvayet cittaṃ ekāgrabhāvena || 973 ||
artha-jaise dhanuṣakā kartā bāṇako milāye donoṃ netroṃkara sarala karatā hai usītaraha sādhu bhī sthira vṛttikara manakā abhyāsa kare || 973 || kammassa baṃdhamokkho jīvājīve ya vvapajjāe| saṃsārasarīrāṇi ya bhogaviratto sayā jhāhi ||974|| karmaṇo baṃdhamokṣau jīvājīvau ca dravyaparyāyān | saṃsāraśarīrāṇi ca bhogaviraktaḥ sadā dhyāya || 974 ||
