﻿________________

348
mūlācāraartha-jñānāvaraṇādi karmake baṃdha mokṣako, jīva ajīva dravyoṃko tathā unakī paryāyoṃko aura saṃsāra tathā śarīrako bhogoṃse virakta huā muni dhyāve || 974 || davve khette kāle bhāve ya bhave ya hoṃti paṃceva | parivaṇāṇi vahuso aṇādikāle ya ciṃtejo ||975||
dravyaṃ kṣetraṃ kālo bhāvaśca bhavaśca bhavaṃti paṃcaiva | parivartanāni bahuśaḥ anādikāle ca ciṃtayet || 975 ||
artha-dravyaparivartana kṣetraparivartana kālaparivartana bhāvaparivartana bhavaparivartana-ye pāṃca parivartana isa jīvane anādikālase lekara anekavāra kiye aisā citavana karanā cāhiye || 975 || mohaggiṇā mahaṃteṇa dajjhamāṇe mahājage dhīrā | samaṇā visayavirattā jhāyaṃti aṇaṃtasaṃsāraṃ ||976||
mohāninā mahatā dahyamānaṃ mahājagat dhīrāḥ | śramaṇā viṣayaviraktā dhyāyaṃti anaṃtasaṃsāraṃ || 976 ||
artha-mahān moharūpī agnise jalate hue saba lokako dekhakara viṣayoṃse virakta dhīramuni anaṃtasaṃsārake kharūpakā citavana karate haiṃ || 976 || āraṃbhaṃ ca kasāyaṃ ca ṇa sahadi tavo tahā loe| acchī lavaṇasamuddo ya kayāraṃ khalu jahā diṭuṃ||977
āraṃbhaṃ ca kaṣāyān ca na sahate tapastathā loke | akṣi lavaṇasamudraśca kacāraṃ khalu yathā dṛṣṭam || 977 ||

samayasārādhikāra 10| 359 upalabdhapuṇyapāpasya tasyopasthāpanā asti || 1010||
artha-jo jinadevakara kahe gaye pṛthivīkāyika jīvoṃkā atyaṃta śraddhāna karatā hai puṇyapāpa jānanevāle usa puruṣake mokṣamārgameṃ sthiti avaśya hai || 1010 || ṇa saddahadi jo ede jīve puḍhavidaṃ gade | sa gacche digghamaddhāṇaṃ liṃgatthobi hu dummadī ||101.1
na śraddadhāti ya etān jīvān pṛthivītvaṃ gatān | sa gacchet dīrghamadhvānaṃ liṃgasthopi hi durmatiḥ 1011 ||
artha-jo pṛthivīpaneko prāpta hue jīvoṃkā śraddhāna nahīṃ karatā vaha nanatva cinhakara sahita bhī durbuddhi dīrgha saṃsārako prāpta hotā hai || 1011|| . kadhaṃ care kadhaṃ ciḍhe kadhamāse kadhaṃ saye| kadhaṃ bhuṃjeja bhāsija kadhaṃ pāvaṃ ṇa vajjhadi ||1012 kathaṃ caret kathaṃ tiṣṭhet kathamāsīta kathaṃ śayīta | kathaṃ jhuṃjīta bhāṣeta kathaṃ pāpaṃ na badhyate || 1012 ||
artha-isa prakāra kahe gaye kramakara jīvoṃse bhare jagatameṃ sādhu kisataraha gamana kare, kaise tiṣṭhe, kaise baiṭhe, kaise soye, kaise bhojana kare, kaise bole, kisa taraha pāpase na baṃdhe ? aisā śiṣyane praśna kiyā || 1012 || ___ aba usakā uttara kahate haiṃjadaṃ care jadaṃ ciṭṭe jadamāse jadaṃ saye| jadaṃ bhuṃjeja bhāseja evaṃ pāvaṃ ṇa bajjhaī ||1013 ||
yataṃ caret yataṃ tiṣṭhet yatamāsīta yataṃ śayīta |

artha—bhavana ādi sarvārthasiddhiparyaṃta jisa vimāna meṃ sīpake puṭake ākāra upapādaśilāke ūpara ratnoṃkara jaḍita saba ābhūṣaṇoṃse śobhita palaṃgapara deva utpanna hotā hai usī jagaha apane yauvanavāle bhūṣita śarīra se samaya samaya prati paryāṣṭha ( pūrṇa ) hotājātā hai || aba dehasūtrakā varṇana karate haiṃ;

373
paryāpti-adhikāra 12| naivāsthīni naiva sirā devānāṃ śarīrasaṃsthāne || 1052 ||
artha-devoṃke śarīrake ākārameṃ bāla nakha ḍāḍhī mūcha roma camaḍa़ā māṃsa lohī mūtra viṣṭhā haḍḍī nasoṃkā jāla-ye saba nahīṃ hote haiṃ || 1052 || varavaṇṇagaṃdharasaphāsā divvaṃ bahupoggalehiṃ ṇimmāṇaṃ | geṇhadi devo dehaṃ sucaridakammāṇubhāveṇa ||1053 || varavarṇagaṃdharasasparśeḥ divyabahupudgalaiśca nirmitaṃ | gṛhṇāti devo dehaṃ sucaritakarmānubhāvena || 1053 ||
artha-jinake śreṣṭa rūpa gaṃdha rasasparśa haiṃ aise divya vaikriyikavargaṇāke anaṃta pudgaloṃse bane hue śarīrako pūrva upārjana kiye śubhakarma ke prabhāvase vaha deva grahaṇa karatā hai || 1053 || vegumviyaṃ sarīraṃ devāṇaṃ māṇusāṇa saṃṭhāṇaṃ | suhaṇāma pasatthagadī sussaravayaṇaṃ surūvaṃ ca||1054||
vaikriyikaṃ śarīraṃ devānāṃ manuṣyāṇāṃ saṃsthānaṃ | śubhanāma praśastagatiḥ sukharavacanaṃ surūpaṃ ca || 1054 ||
artha-devoṃkā śarīra vikriyāyukta honese vaikriyika hai manuṣyoṃ ke samāna pahalā samacaturasra saṃsthāna hotā hai, śubhanāma praśastagamana sukharavacana surūpa ye bhī hote haiṃ || 1054 || paḍhamāe puḍhavīe raiyāṇaṃ tu hoi usseho| sattadhaṇu tiṇṇirayaṇī chacceva ya aṃgulā hoti||1055 prathamāyāṃ pṛthivyāṃ nairayikāṇāṃ tu bhavati utsedhaḥ | sapta dhanūṃṣi triratnayaḥ ṣaṭ eva ca aṃgulā bhavaṃti ||1055||

374
mūlācāra
artha-pahalī ratnaprabhā nāmā narakakī pṛthivīmeṃ nārakiyoṃkī uṃcāī sāta dhanuṣa tīna hātha chaha aṃgula pramāṇa hai || 1055 || bidiyāe puḍhavīe ṇeraiyāṇaṃ tu hoi usseho| paṇṇarasa doṇi bārasa dhaṇu radaṇī aṃgulā ceva1056
dvitīyāyāṃ pṛthivyāṃ nārakāṇāṃ tu bhavati utsedhaḥ | paṃcadaśa dvau dvādaśa dhanūṃṣi ratnayaḥ aṃgulāścaiva || 1056 ||
artha- śarkarā pṛthivīmeṃ nārakiyoṃke śarīrakī uṃcāī paṃdraha dhanuṣa do hātha bāraha aṃgula pramāṇa hai || 1056 || tadiyāe puḍhavīe raiyāṇaṃ tu hoi usseho| ekattīsaṃ ca dhaṇū egā radaṇī muṇeyavvā || 1057 ||
tṛtīyāyāṃ pṛthivyāṃ nārakāṇāṃ tu bhavati utsedhaḥ| ekatriṃśaca dhanūṃṣi ekā raniḥ maṃtavyā ||1057 ||
artha-bālukā pṛthivīmeṃ nārakiyoṃke śarīrakī uṃcāī ikatīsa dhanuṣa eka hātha jānanā cāhiye || 1057 || cauthīe puḍhavīe ṇeraiyāṇaṃ tu hoi usseho| bāsaṭṭhī ceva dhaṇū be radaṇī hoṃti ṇāyavvā || 1058||
caturthī pṛthivyāṃ nārakāṇāṃ tu bhavati utsedhaḥ | dvāṣaṣṭiḥ caiva dhanūṃṣi dve ratnī bhavaṃti jñātavyāḥ ||1058||
artha-paṃkaprabhā pṛthivīmeṃ nārakiyoṃkī uṃcāī bāsaṭha dhanuṣa do hātha pramāṇa hai aisā jānanā || 1058 || paṃcamie puḍhavīe raiyāṇaṃ tu hoi usseho | sadamegaṃ paṇavīsaṃ dhaṇuppamāṇeṇa ṇādavvaṃ || 1059|| paṃcamyāṃ pṛthivyāṃ nārakāṇāṃ tu bhavati utsedhaḥ|

mūlācāra
vyaṃtaradevoṃkā śarīra dasa dhanuṣa ūṃcā hai aura jyotiṣī devoṃkā sāta dhanuṣa ūṃcā hai || 1062 || chaddhaṇusahassussedhaṃ cadu dugamicchaṃti bhogabhūmīsu | paṇavīsaṃ paṃcasadā vodhavvā kammabhūmīsu||1063 ||
ṣaṭ dhanuḥsahasrotsedhaṃ catvāri dve icchaṃti bhogabhūmiṣu | paṃcaviṃśatiḥ paṃcaśatāni boddhavyāni karmabhūmiṣu ||1063||
artha-bhogabhūmiyoṃmeṃ uttama madhyama jaghanya bhogabhūmike manuṣyoṃkī uṃcāī kramase chaha hajāra dhanuṣa cāra hajāra dhanuṣa do hajāra dhanuṣa pramāṇa hai| aura karmabhūmike manuṣyoṃkī utkṛṣṭa uṃcāī pāṃcasau paccīsa dhanuṣapramāṇa hai || 1063 || sohammīsāṇesu ya devā khalu hoṃti sattarayaṇīo| chacceva ya rayaṇīo saṇakumāre hi māhiṃde ||1064 ||
saudharmeśānayośca devāḥ khalu bhavaṃti sapta ratnayaḥ | ṣaṭ caiva ca ratnayaḥ sanatkumāre hi māhiṃdre || 1064 ||
artha-saudharma aura aiśāna vargake deva sāta hātha ūṃce hote haiṃ | sanatkumāra aura māheṃdra vargake chaha hātha ūṃce haiṃ ||1064 || baṃbhe ya laṃtavevi ya kappe khalu hoṃti paṃca rayaṇīo| cattāri ya rayaṇīo sukkasahassārakappesu||1065|| brahme ca lāṃtavepi ca kalpe khalu bhavaṃti paṃcaratnayaḥ| catvāraśca ratnayaḥ śukrasahasrārakalpeṣu ||1065||
artha-brahma yugala aura lāṃtava yugalameṃ pāṃca hātha ūṃce hote haiṃ aura śukra yugala tathā śatāra sahasrāra svargameṃ cāra hātha ūṃce hote haiṃ || 1065 ||

artha-ghanāṃgula (dravyāṃgula ) ke asaṃkhyātaveṃ bhāga pramāṇa ekeṃdriyase lekara paṃceṃdrī tiryaṃcoṃtaka jaghanya deha hotā hai||1069 sāhiyasahassameyaṃ tu joyaṇāṇaṃ haveja ukkassaṃ | eiṃdiyassa dehaṃ taṃ puṇa paumatti ṇādavvaṃ || 1070 ||

paryāpti-adhikāra 12 | 379 dve caiva yojanaśate saptaviṃśatiśca bhavaṃti boddhavyāni||1072 trīṇyeva gavyūtīni aṣṭāviṃśatiśca dhanuḥśataṃ bhaṇitaṃ | trayodaśa aṃgulāni ardhāṃgulameva saviśeṣa || 1073 ||
artha-lākha yojana vistāravāle jaṃbūdvīpakī paridhi (golāī) tīna lākha solaha hajāra dosau sattāīsa yojana tīna kosa ekasau aṭṭhāīsa dhanuṣa sāḍhe teraha aṃgula kucha adhika ( eka jau pramāṇa ) hai || 1072-1073 || jaṃbūdīvo dhādaikhaṃḍo pukkharavaro ya taha dīvo| vāruṇivara khīravaro ya ghidavaro khodavaradīvo||1074 ṇaṃdīsaro ya aruṇo aruṇabhāso ya kuṃḍalavaro ya | saṃkhavararujagabhujagavarakusavarakuṃcavaradīvo ||1075||
jaṃbūdvīpo dhātakīkhaṃḍaḥ puṣkaravaraśca tathā dvīpaḥ| vāruṇivaraḥ kṣīravaraśca ghṛtavaraḥ kṣaudravaradvīpaḥ ||1074 || naṃdīśvaraśva aruṇaḥ aruṇābhāsaśca kuṃḍalavaraśca | śaṃkhavararucakabhujagavarakuśavarakrauṃcavaradvīpaḥ || 1075 ||
artha-pahalā jaṃbūdvīpa dhātakīkhaṃḍa puṣkaravaradvīpa vāruṇīvara kṣīravara ghṛtavara kṣaudravara naṃdīśvara aruṇa aruṇābhāsa kuṃḍalavara śaṃkhavara rucakadvīpa bhujagavara kuśavara kauṃcavara dvīpa solahavāṃ hai || 1074-1075|| evaṃ dīvasamuddā duguṇaduguṇavitthaḍā asaṃkhejā| ede du tiriyaloe sayaṃbhuramaṇodahī jāva || 1076 ||
evaṃ dvīpasamudrā dviguṇadviguṇavistṛtā asaṃkhyātāḥ | ete tu tiryagloke svayaṃbhūramaṇodadheḥ yāvat || 1076 ||

mūlācāra__artha-isa prakāra dvīpa samudra dūne dūne vistāravāle haiṃ asaṃkhyāta haiṃ | ye dvīpasamudrādika khayaṃbhūramaṇa samudraparyaṃta haiṃ aura tiryaglokameṃ haiṃ || 1076 || jāvadiyā uddhārā aḍḍāijāṇa sāgaruvamāṇaṃ | tāvadiyā khalu romā havaṃti dīvā samuddā ya||1077

artha-aḍhāī sāgaropamake jitane uddhārapalya haiṃ unameṃ jitane roma haiṃ utane hī dvīpa samudra haiṃ || 1077 || jaṃbūdīve lavaṇo dhādaikhaṃḍe ya kālaudadhī ya | sesāṇaṃ dīvāṇaṃ dīvasarisaṇāmayā uddhī ||1078|| jaṃbūdvīpe lavaṇo dhātakikhaṃḍe ca kālodadhiśca | śeṣāṇāṃ dvīpānāṃ dvīpasadṛśanāmāna udadhayaḥ || 1078 || artha-jaṃbūdvīpameṃ lavaṇa samudra hai ghātakīkhaṃḍameṃ kālodadhi samudra hai aura śeṣa ( bākī) dvīpoṃmeṃ dvīpoṃke nāma samāna nāmavāle samudra haiṃ || 1078 || patteyarasā cattāri sāyarā tiṇṇi hoṃti udayarasā| avasesā ya samuddā khodarasā hoṃti ṇāyavvā||1079 pratyekarasāḥ catvāraḥ sāgarāḥ trayo bhavaṃti udakarasāḥ | avaśeṣāśca samudrāḥ kṣaudrarasā bhavaṃti jñātavyāḥ ||1079||

artha - sparśana iṃdriyakā viṣaya cārasau dhanuṣa hai, rasanā iṃdri yakā viṣaya cauṃsaṭha dhanuṣa hai, ghrāṇa iṃdriyakā viṣaya sau dhanuṣa hai | ekeṃdriya se lekara asaṃjñipaṃceṃdriya paryaṃta jīvoṃke sparśana ādikā viṣaya āge āge dūnā dūnā kahā hai || 1092 || guṇatīsajoyaṇasadāī caḍavaṇṇāya hoi ṇāyavvā | cauriṃdiyassa ṇiyamā cakkhupphāsaṃ viyāṇā hi 1093 ekonatriṃśat yojanaśatāni catuḥ paṃcāśat bhavati jñātavyāni | caturiṃdriyasya niyamāt cakṣuḥsparśaḥ vijānīhi || 1093 || artha – cauiṃdriya jīvake cakṣu iṃdriyakā viṣaya unatīsasau cauvana yojana pramāṇa jānanā || 1093 || uṇasaṭṭhi joyaṇasadā aṭṭheva ya hoṃti taha ya ṇāyavvā | asaṇṇipaceṃdīe cakkhupphāsaṃ viyāṇāhi || 1094 || ekonaṣaṣṭiyojanaśatāni aṣṭaiva ca bhavaṃti tathā ca jñātavyāni | asaṃjñipaṃceṃdriyasya cakṣuḥ sparśa vijānāhi || 1094 ||

tataḥ paraṃ tu niyamāt upapādo nāsti anyaliṃgānāṃ | nigraṃthaśrāvakāṇāṃ upapādaḥacyutaṃ yāvat || 1174 ||
artha–sahasrārase āgeke vargoṃ meṃ anyaliṃgiyoṃkā janma nahīṃ hotā digaṃbara śrāvaka śrāvikā āryikāoṃkā janma acyuta vargataka hotā hai || 1174 || jābuvarimagevejaṃ uvavādo abhaviyāṇa ukkasso| ukkaṭeṇa taveṇa duṇiyamā ṇiggaṃthaliṃgeṇa || 1175 ||
yāvat uparimauveyaṃ upapādaḥ abhavyānāṃ utkṛṣṭaḥ | utkṛṣṭena tapasā tu niyamāt nigraṃthaliṃgena || 1175 || artha-abhavyoṃkā janma nirmathaliṃga dhāraṇakara utkṛṣṭa tapa

paryāpti-adhikāra 12| 413 cayakara kadācit mokṣa jāte haiṃ tīrthaṃkara baladeva cakravartīpaneko bhī kadācit pāte haiṃ athavā nahīṃ bhī pāte || 1181 ||: savvaṭṭhādo ya cudā bhanjā titthayaracakkavaṭṭitte | rāmattaṇeṇa bhajjā ṇiyamā puṇa ṇivvudi jaṃti.||1182 sarvārthāca cyutā bhājyāḥ tīrthakaracakravartitvena | rāmatvena bhājyā niyamāt punaḥ nirvṛtiṃ yāṃti ||1182||

paryāpti-adhikāra 12| 415. sthāna sūtra cauthā caudahaguṇasthāna sūtra pāṃcavāṃ caudaha mārgaṇāsūtraina pāṃca sūtroṃse sthānasūtrakā vyākhyāna karate haiṃ || 1187 || gadiādimaggaṇāo paravidāo ya coddasā ceva | edesiṃ khalu bhedā kiṃci samāseṇa vocchāmi||1988

ekeṃdriyādayaḥ jīvāḥ paṃcavidhā bhagavatā du prajñaptāḥ | pṛthivīkāyādayaḥ vikalāḥ paṃceṃdriyā eva || 1189 ||

śaṃkho gobhī bhramarādayastu vikaleṃdriyā jñātavyāḥ| paṃceṃdriyāstu jalasthalakhacarāḥ suranārakanarāśca || 1190||
artha-śaṃkhādi gopālikā ādi bhorā ādi kramase doiṃdrī teiṃdrī cauiṃdrī jānanā aura jalacara sthalacara ākāśacara tathā deva nārakī manuṣya-ye saba paṃceṃdriya jānane || 1190 || paṃcaya iṃdiyapāṇā maṇavacakāyā du tiṇṇi balapāṇā|

416

paryāpti-adhikāra 12| coṃmeṃ pāṃca guṇasthāna haiṃ aura manuṣyagatimeṃ caudaha guṇasthāna pāye jāte haiṃ || 1200 || eiṃdiyāya paṃceṃdiyā ya uḍḍamahatiriyaloesu| sayalavigaliṃdiyā puṇa jīvā tiriyaṃmi loyaṃmi || ekeṃdriyāḥ paṃceṃdriyāśca ūrdhvamadhastiryaglokeṣu | sakalavikaleṃdriyāḥ punaḥ jīvāḥ tiryagloke || 1201||

mūlācārasiddhehiṃ aṇaṃtaguṇā saveṇavi tīdakāleṇa || 1204||

artha-eka nigoda śarīra (sādhāraṇa vanaspatī) meṃ jīva apane dravyapramāṇase siddhoṃse anaṃtaguṇe aura saba atītakālase anaṃtaguṇe haiṃ aisā bhagavānane dekhā hai || 1204 || eiṃdiyā aṇaṃtā vaṇapphadīkāyigā ṇigodesu | puḍhavī āū teū vāū loyā asaṃkhijjā || 1205||
ekeṃdriyā anaṃtā vanaspatikāyikā nigodeṣu | pṛthvī āpaḥ tejaḥ vāyavaḥ lokā asaṃkhyātāḥ ||1205||
artha-nigodoṃmeṃ vanaspatikāyika ekeṃdriya jīva anaṃtānaṃta haiṃ aura pṛthivīkāya jalakāya tejaḥkāya vāyukāyika jīva asaṃkhyāta loka pramāṇa haiṃ || 1205|| tasakāiyā asaṃkhā seḍhīo padarachedaṇippaṇṇā | sesāsu maggaṇāsuvi ṇedavvā jīva samāseja ||1206 trasakāyikā asaṃkhyātāḥ śreṇyaḥ pratarachedaniṣpannāḥ| śeṣāsu mārgaṇāsvapi netavyā jīvāḥ samāśritya ||1206|| artha-do iṃdriya ādi trasa jīva loka pratarake bhāga karanese utpanna asaṃkhyāta śreṇī mātra haiṃ | isa prakāra śeṣa mārgaṇāoṃmeṃ bhī jīvoṃko āśrayakara saṃkhyā jānanī || 1206 ||
aba kuloṃkā kathana karanā cāhiye thā paraṃtu paṃcācārādhikārameṃ 221 veṃ gāthāse lekara 225 veṃ gāthātaka vyākhyāna

121
kiyā gayā hai isase yahāṃ cāra gāthā punarukta doṣa ke bhayase do bāra nahīṃ likhe isaliye khādhyāyavāle 96 veṃke patra meṃ dekhaleṃ || āge alpa bahutvako kahate haiṃ;

mūlācāratiṇhaṃ khaluṃ paḍhamāṇaṃ ukkassaṃ aṃtarāyayasseya | tīsaṃ koḍākoḍī sāyaraṇāmāṇameva ṭhidī||1237||
trayāṇāṃ khalu prathamānāṃ utkṛṣṭa aṃtarāyasyaiva | triṃśat koṭīkovyaḥ sāgaranāmnāmeva sthitiḥ || 1237 ||
artha-pahale tīna jñānāvaraṇī darśanāvaraṇī vedanīya aura aṃtarāya ina cāra karmoṃkī utkṛṣṭasthiti (rahanekā kāla) tīsa koḍa़ākoḍī sāgara pramāṇa hai || 1237 || mohassa sattaraṃ khalu vīsaṃ ṇāmassa ceva godassa | tetīsamāugāṇaṃ uvamāo sāgarāṇaṃ tu || 1238||
mohasya saptatiḥ khalu viṃśatiḥ nāmnaḥ caiva gotrasya | trayastriṃśat āyuṣa upamāḥ sāgarāṇāṃ tu || 1238 || artha-mohanīya mithyātvakī sattara koḍākoḍa़ī hai nāmakarma aura gotrakarmakī utkṛṣṭasthiti vīsa koḍākoḍī sāgaropama hai aura āyukarmakī utkṛṣṭasthiti tetīsa sāgaropamakī hai ||1238|| bārasa ya vedaṇīe ṇāmāgodāṇamaṭṭhaya muhuttā| bhiṇṇamuhuttaṃ tu ṭhidī jahaṇṇayaṃ sesa paṃcaṇhaṃ ||1239||
dvādaśa ca vedanīyasya nāmagotrayoraṣṭau muhūrtāḥ | bhinnamuhūrta tu sthitiḥ jaghanyā śeṣāṇāṃ paṃcānāṃ ||1239|| artha-vedanīyakarmakī jaghanyasthiti bārahamuhūrtakī hai nāma aura gotra ina do karmoṃkī āṭha muhūrta hai aura bākīke jñānāvaraṇādi pāṃca karmoṃkī jaghanyasthiti aṃtarmuhūrtapramāṇa hai || 1239 || kammāṇaṃ jo duraso ajjhavasāṇajaṇida suha asuhovā baṃdho so aṇubhāgo padesabaṃdho imo hoi || 1240 ||

paryāpti - adhikāra 12 |
431
karmaṇāṃ yastu rasa adhyavasānajanitaḥ śubho'śubho vā | baṃdhaḥ saḥ anubhāgaḥ pradeśabaṃdha ḥ ayaṃ bhavati || 1240 || artha — jñānāvaraṇādi karmoṃkā jo kaṃṣāyādi pariṇāmajanita śubha athavā aśubha rasa ( phaladānaśakti ) hai vaha anubhāgabaṃdha hai | tathā pradeśabaṃdhakā svarūpa aba āge kahate haiṃ || 1240 || sume jogaviseseṇa egakhettāvagāḍhaṭhidiyāṇaṃ | ekeke dupade se kammapadesā aṇaṃtā du || 1241 || sūkṣmā yogaviśeṣāt eka kṣetrāvagāḍhasthitāḥ | ekaikasmin tu pradeśe karmapradeśā anaṃtāstu || 1241 || artha – manavacanakāyakī kriyārūpa yogaviśeṣase eka hī jagahameṃ sthita ātmāke eka eka pradeśapara virājamāna sūkṣma jñānāvaraṇādi karmaparamāṇū anaṃta haiṃ | 1241 || yahāṃ taka karmabaṃdhakā svarūpa kahā |
āge karmoṃ ke kṣaya hone kā krama kahate haiṃ; - mohassāvaraṇāṇaṃ khayeṇa aha aṃtarāyassa ya eva | uvavajjai kevalayaṃ payāsayaṃ savvabhāvāṇaṃ || 1242 || mohasyāvaraṇayoḥ kṣayeṇa atha aṃtarāyasya caiva | utpadyate kevalaṃ prakāśakaṃ sarvabhāvānāṃ || 1242 ||
artha — mohanīya karma aura jñānāvaraṇa darśanāvaraṇa aṃtarāyakarma ina cāra ghātiyā karmoke nāśa honese saba padārthoṃ ko prakāśanevālā aisā kevalajñāna pragaṭa hotā hai | 1242 ||
āge kevalī honeke bāda karmakṣaya honekā vidhāna kahate haiṃ;tattorāliyadeho ṇāmā godaṃ ca kevalī yugavaṃ |

432
mūlācāraāūga vedaṇīyaṃ caduhiṃ khiviittu ṇīraohoi ||1243 tata audārikadehaṃ nāma gotraṃ ca kevalī yugapat | āyuḥ vedanīyaṃ catvāri kṣapayitvā nīrajā bhavati ||1243|| artha-yoganirodha karake ayoga kevalī honeke bāda ve ayoga kevalī jina audārika śarīrasahita nāmakarma, gotrakarma āyukarma aura vedanīyakarma ina cāra aghātiyā kokā kṣayakara karmarūpī rajarahita nirmala siddha bhagavāna ho jāte haiṃ|
bhāvārtha-ayogakevalī apane kālake dūsare aṃtasamayameṃ bahattari karmaprakṛtiyoṃkā kṣaya karate haiṃ phira aṃtake samayameṃ teraha prakṛtiyoṃkā nāśakara śarīra choḍa़ nirmala saba upādhiyoṃse rahita anaṃtaguṇamayī siddha paramātmā hue mokṣasthānameṃ sadā virājate haiṃ || 1243 ||
isaprakāra ācāryaśrīvaṭṭakeriviracita mūlācārakī hiṃdībhāṣāṭīkāmeṃ paryāpti ādiko kahane. vālā bāravāṃ paryāpti-adhikāra
